## 0.5.44

- For WoW: Forever 1.60.1 build 70938. The interface number stays 16001.

## 0.5.43

- For WoW: Forever 1.60.1 build 70938. The interface number stays 16001.
- Threat list: a new List row. At Enemies while you tank (the default), a tank sees a row for each enemy with a nameplate that you or your group fight, instead of the group. Each row has the enemy's raid marker, its name and a bar of your hold on it, and the ones you are losing come first.
- Threat list: in Enemies, an enemy someone else holds is Lost. It is red, pulses fast and names who holds it in their class colour with their share of your threat ("Mage 112%"). One where another member passes Slipping at (80% by default) turns amber and pulses slowly, naming who is closing in. Enemies you hold stay green, calm and a little dimmer.
- Threat list: Enemies outlines your target's row, and the title reads "Threat: 3 enemies, 1 lost". A last line counts the enemies past Number of bars, and an enemy that turns to Lost flashes and plays Sound when you lose an enemy (by default Warning sound, while Warn when you lose aggro is on, as in the group list). An enemy another tank holds stays calm.
- Threat list: Enemies reads five times a second in a fight and nothing out of one. Its rows take no clicks; pointing at one says who has the enemy and to click its nameplate. Enemies you fight shows it in every role, and Your group on your target keeps the list as it was.
- Threat list: Animate bars (on by default). Bars slide to each new value, rows glide to their new place and flash as they overtake another, the top bar has a faint sheen, and your group's names take a light shade of their class colour.
- Threat list: test mode and /flatui threat show four sample enemies in Enemies, one lost, one slipping and two held, and in test mode their states turn every 6 seconds.
- Nameplates: Mark enemies you are losing (on by default). While you tank, an enemy changing hands or on someone else pulses with a soft glow in its threat colour, faster once it is lost, under a small arrow pointing down at it. The sample nameplates of test mode and Setup show it too.
- New **Absorb shields on the health bar** (Bright layer over the health), in a new **Absorb shields** section of the **Personal resource display** page. Your absorb shields now lie over the display's health bar as a silvery layer that starts where your health ends, with a thin bright edge where the shield ends. A shield bigger than your missing health runs on back over your health, so its full size still shows on the bar; at full health it runs in from the right end. **Plain fill after the health** stops it at the end of the bar instead, and **Off** takes it away.
- The shield layer has a slow **Sheen** (on) that sweeps across it every few seconds. It flashes as a new shield lands or grows and fades quickly as it breaks, though not while the game withholds the amount, and **Shield outline** (on) frames the whole health bar in **Shield colour** while a shield is up. **Shield colour** (a light silver blue) and **Shield opacity** (60%) set its look, and **Shield amount** (off) adds the amount after the health text, as +1,234, in the shield colour.
- The shield on the health bar works in a fight and uses the game's absorb total. On a client without one it shows the shields the **Absorb shields** page tracks. Test mode lays a sample shield of about 35% of your health over the display, with its sheen, and setup's **Personal resource display** step lists the new rows.
- New **What's new** window: from now on, the first login after Flat UI updates shows what changed in each version since the one you last played on this account, newest first, with setting names in the accent colour. This first time it shows 0.5.43, as older versions kept no note of the one you played. It has **Open Flat UI's options** and **Close**, Escape closes it, and it can be dragged anywhere on screen. It waits for the end of a fight, and if a fight starts it steps aside ("Flat UI: What's new comes back after the fight.") and comes back after it. A new install shows nothing, as the welcome screen and setup walk you through Flat UI.
- New **What's new after an update**, under **Updates** on the **Options and setup** tab of the **Flat UI** page: **Show the window** (the default), **One line in chat** ("Flat UI updated to 0.5.43. /flatui new shows what changed.") or **Nothing**. A character the welcome screen greets gets the chat line instead of the window.
- New `/flatui new` (or `/flatui changes`) opens **What's new** at any time with this version's changes, and `/flatui new 0.5.41` shows that version, one of the newest six. **What's new in 0.5.43**, at the bottom of **Home** in the options window, opens it too.

## 0.5.42

- For WoW: Forever 1.60.1 build 70938. The interface number stays 16001.
- `/flatui scale 80` now sets the scale it names. At the defaults it did nothing, because **Scale below 65%** (on by default) sets the scale before **Custom scale**. From 65 to 115 it now turns **Pixel-perfect scale** and **Scale below 65%** off and uses **Custom scale**; from 35 to 64 it uses **Small scale** with Scale below 65% on. Chat then gives the real result in the words of `/flatui status`, as "Flat UI: Custom UI scale: 80%. /reload puts the game's scale back.", and the reload notice shows when Scale below 65% goes off. `/flatui scale off` answers the same way.
- `/flatui` followed by words that are no command, as `/flatui border colour`, now opens the options window with those words in **Search**: the results show lit, Enter opens the first, and a miss shows the closest pages. Before, it printed the whole command list. `/flatui help` and `/flatui ?` still list the commands.
- Setup's **Your screen is set** card can now take a **Finish** back until you reload. After a save into the profile in use it shows **Undo setup**, which puts that profile back as it was, as **Bring back** does ("Flat UI: Raid is back as it was before setup.", with "Game settings setup changed stay as they are." when it wrote some). A Finish that made a new profile now shows **Back to Default**, as a ready-made look does. The Finish line in chat ends "Undo setup on the card, or Profiles > Bring back, puts it back until you reload."
- Home's **Needs a reload** **Undo** now keeps the change in the record. **Recent changes** shows the Undo as a line of its own, and Ctrl+Z (or that line's **Undo**) makes the change again, as the reload notice's Undo does. Before, the change vanished from Recent changes and Ctrl+Z stepped back to an older one. The strip now ends "Ctrl+Z makes it again."
- The small reset arrow on a changed row now says what it did in the strip: "Reset Border colour: back to #000000. Ctrl+Z puts it back." ("back to its default" where the value has no words of its own). The arrow brightens under the mouse, as the **Search** box's cross does.
- The questions and tips of **Delete profile**, **Reset profile**, **Copy from** and an import with **Replace** no longer say "for good". Each now ends "Bring back puts it back until you reload." (or "them"), as in 'Delete "Alt"? No other character uses it. Bring back puts it back until you reload.' They stay red, and Enter still does not answer them.
- Dragging **Custom scale**, or changing a row of the **Fonts** page such as **Text size** or **Interface font**, in a fight now says so, once a fight, while the options window shows: "Flat UI: the UI scale changes when the fight ends." or "Flat UI: fonts change when the fight ends.". The change still lands as the fight ends.
- The sidebar now shows where your changes are: a small dot in the accent after the name of each module whose pages (its tabs and **Per frame**) hold a setting off its default. The dot goes once the last of them is back at its default. The **Icon rail** layout shows no names and no dots.
- The **Flat UI** page now has a preview band: setup's bordered panel with a health and a mana bar, in your border, background and **Bar texture**. Its **Colours** tab shows setup's colour bars: health at 100%, 70%, 40% and 15%, the cast, channel, uninterruptible, interrupted and craft colours, reaction and power. Both follow each change at once.
- **Switch automatically**: **Switch now** in a fight now waits for the fight's end, as a profile picked by hand does. The strip says "Switches to Raid when the fight ends.", the title bar shows "Raid after the fight", and the profile switches as the fight ends. Before, it only said "Profiles change after combat." and nothing waited.
- New **Fade as one runs out** (on), under **Squares for your heals over time**. Each square now fades from full to about a third over the last 3 seconds of its heal over time, so a healer sees which Renew or Rejuvenation to refresh next. It goes back to full when the spell is recast. In a fight it fades by the end time read before the pull, and a square that never ends stays full. Setup's Bex shows the last square half faded.
- Heal over time squares on party frames draw at the default **Square size** 6 again at one pixel a unit. Since 0.5.41 they drew as 5, which left almost half less colour inside the rim. A square now stops one pixel short of the bar's edge, so sizes 7 to 14 picked for raid cells still stay inside the 10-unit party bar. Setup's Bex matches.
- New **Five-second rule** (off), on the **Player frame** tab. After each spell that costs mana, a thin bright spark crosses your mana bar from left to right over 5 seconds and starts again at the next cast. When it reaches the end, Spirit is refilling your mana again. It works in a fight, on the game's bar or Flat UI's, and a druid in Bear or Cat Form sees it on the druid mana bar. Test mode loops it, and setup's **Player and target frames** step draws it 40% of the way across the mana bar.
- **Dim members out of range** now darkens only the member's own picture, name row and bars (their body in **One frame**). Before, it also darkened the top of their debuff icons and pet frame, which left a half-dark strip. A party pet's frame now dims by its own range. With **Border for your target** at Own colour, the outline wraps the same part, just clear of the picture's border, and no longer runs through the pet frame and the debuff row.
- With **Picture** None in **Separate parts**, as the **Healer** look sets it, the party marks now use the empty picture square. The raid marker sits at its top, the resurrection icon at full size in its middle and AFK at its bottom, clear of the name row and the health text. In **One frame**, where None removes the square, they stay on the health bar.
- **AFK on party frames** now fades in and out. After the first minute it counts the time away, "AFK 3m" and later "AFK 1h", from when Flat UI first saw the member go, so a group waiting at the boss knows whether to wait or pull.
- New **AFK on raid frames** (on). Raid cells and raid-style party frames show the same soft grey AFK, fading and counting the same way, where the game's status text sits and at its size. It works in a fight too and hides while the member is dead or offline. Setup's **Boss and raid frames** step shows it on Eli.
- Setup's **Party frames** step now draws **AFK on party frames**. Dru, already drawn out of range, shows a grey AFK at the bottom of his picture, or at the left of his bar with **Picture** None in **One frame**, and pointing at the row brackets it. The row moved there from **Auras, flags and icons**.
- Setup: **Role icons for** moved to the **Party frames** step. Cole, Dru and Fay lose their icons with Tanks and healers, and Mara loses hers with Healers only. The bracket is on Bex's healer icon, which every choice keeps. Before, picking a choice in **Auras, flags and icons** changed nothing in its drawing.
- Some changes made in a fight now say once in chat, while the options window is open, that they apply when the fight ends, as the heal over time squares already did. This covers **Dim members out of range** ("Flat UI: party members dim by range when the fight ends."), **Low health border**, a **Health text** or **Power text** format, and the party and raid marks: the AFK words, **Resurrection icon on party frames**, the raid markers, the leader icons and **Border for your target** ("Flat UI: party and raid frame marks change when the fight ends."). Each change still applies at the end of the fight. A change raised with the window closed, such as zoning or a new member, stays silent.
- New **Flash as you change target** (on), under **Target and focus** on the **Marks and icons** tab of **Nameplates**: as you change target, the new target's health bar border flashes once in **Target colour**. It is a thicker outline that fades over about a third of a second and leaves the normal target border, also in a fight, so with Tab in a pack you see at once which nameplate it took. Setting a focus flashes the focus's nameplate in **Focus colour**. Setup's Target and Focus samples flash as the drawing opens and as the row turns on.
- Rogues: the Overpower row is now **Overpower, Mongoose Bite or Riposte on enemy nameplates**. A rogue who knows **Riposte** sees its icon pop over their target's nameplate as it becomes usable after they parry. It glows for its 5 seconds (a parry of yours renews them), is grey while only energy is short, follows a change of target and goes as you cast it, with the same swipe, pop and fade as Mongoose Bite. Setup's **Marks and icons** drawing shows it for a rogue who knows Riposte.
- New **Nameplates on enemy pets and totems** (off), **Nameplates on minor enemies** (off) and **Friendly players as names only** (off), under **Which show** on the **Size and clicks** tab of **Nameplates**. These are the game's own settings, so the clutter of totems and hunter pets, or friendly players' health bars, can go without leaving Flat UI. Each shows the game's value and writes nothing until you move it, as the other game rows of that tab do.
- Setup's **Nameplate auras** and **Nameplate text** steps, and the preview bands of the **Auras** and **Text** tabs, now draw **Show auras** and **Show health text**. At **Only on the target** the auras and the health number stay on a **Your target** sample alone, and the plain enemy beside it goes bare. **On the target and focus** adds a **Focus** sample that keeps them. Pointing at either row brackets the sample that keeps them.
- Setup's **Marks and icons** drawing for a rogue or druid has a second combo sample, **Full points**: all five squares, in **Full points colour** while **Full points stand out** is on. Dragging the colour repaints it at once, the row pops together as **Full points stand out** turns on, and pointing at either row brackets it.
- Setup's **Nameplate colours and threat** step and the **Threat** tab's preview band now draw a fifth sample, **Held by another tank**, while you count as a tank with **Enemies held by another tank** on. It is in **Another tank's colour**, fades like an enemy held for you, has no ring at **When the enemy needs you** and shows 60%. Pointing at **Enemies held by another tank** or **Another tank's colour** brackets it.
- Fix: with **Your role** at **From your role, stance or form**, setup's threat samples now work your role out afresh (group role, Main Tank, a tank aura, Defensive Stance, Bear Form, Righteous Fury). They show the colours your nameplates will take after **Finish**, not those of the role you had picked before.
- Fix: turning **Restyle friendly nameplates** off or on in a fight now says in chat, once a fight while the options window shows, "Flat UI: nameplates take their new size when the fight ends.", as the other size rows do. Before, the change seemed to do nothing until the fight ended.
- With **Show auras** at **Only on the target** or **On the target and focus**, each change of target or focus now eases the old row of auras out and the new one in over 0.15 s, as the threat rings and combo points fade, so Tab no longer makes the debuff rows blink. A nameplate that shows and a change of the options still set them at once.
- Tips for the **Nameplates** rows that had none: **Restyle friendly nameplates**, **Own border and background**, **Incoming heals**, **Absorb shields**, **Heal absorbs**, **Colour health bars by health left**, **Only in dungeons and raids**, **Show threat value**, the three **Enemy's target** rows, **Level box shape**, **Level box gap**, **Class icon size**, **Raid marker**, **Arrow for nameplates behind you**, **Shield on uninterruptible casts**, **Aura opacity**, **Another tank's colour**, **Extra clickable width** and **Extra clickable height**. Each says in one sentence what it changes.
- Cast bars: the spell icon left of your cast bar and your pet's, with **Spell icon on player and pet bars**, now stays on the bar through its finish fade and its 1.3 s Interrupted hold, with or without **Flat fill**, so you still see which spell was kicked. Before, it went the moment the cast ended, and no icon showed during the Interrupted hold. The latency zone still goes as the cast ends.
- **Target of enemy casts**: the "> Bex" past the target or focus cast bar now stays while the bar fades or holds Interrupted, so you can still read who the kicked Polymorph was aimed at. It goes as the bar hides or the next cast starts. Setup's picture and test mode keep the icon, the name and the pushback in the Interrupted state too, as the live bars do.
- New **Seconds until your interrupt is ready** (on), on the **Text and icon** tab of **Cast bars**: while an enemy you target or focus casts and your Kick, Counterspell or Pummel cools down, a small "4" counts down inside the left end of its cast bar in **Interrupt on cooldown colour**. You see at a glance whether Kick is back before the 3.5 s Fireball lands, and the number goes as the bar turns **Interrupt ready colour**. It works with or without **Flat fill**, in a fight too, while **Interrupt readiness on enemy casts** is on, and setup's **Focus** row draws a sample.
- New **Channel tick colour** (white at first) under **Channel ticks on the player bar**: the tick marks of Mind Flay, Arcane Missiles or Drain Life take it at 60% opacity on the live bar, in setup's picture and in test mode. A priest with **Class colour on the player bar** can now pick a dark colour that shows on the white bar.
- Combat text: with **Spell names** on **After every spell**, a named number in the Outgoing area now takes a lane as wide as its name, and only the two lanes away from your character. An Arcane Explosion, Multi-Shot, Consecration or Hellfire tick on four enemies now reads as two clean columns instead of names printed over each other, and the rest wait their turn.
- New **Sound when your focus casts** (None at first, with the same eight choices), under **Sound** on the **Cast bars** page: it plays as the enemy you focus starts a cast or channel while your interrupt is ready and in range, at most once a second, so you hear "kick now" without watching the focus bar. It needs **Interrupt readiness on enemy casts**, and picking a sound plays it once.
- Combat text: test mode's Outgoing sample and `/flatui combat test` now write "824 Fireball" (the client's own name of the spell) while **Whose hits** is **Yours** and **Spell names** is **After every spell**, as setup's picture does. Pointing at **Spell names** in setup now marks the 510 Fireball sample.
- Combat alerts: **Low health** and **Low mana** now breathe in step with **No target**, one shared cycle, instead of dimming while the other brightens. As the fight ends they fade out over a quarter second with No target instead of vanishing in one frame, so **-Combat** pops in a calm screen.
- Test mode: the first sample Interrupted of the player cast bar in a session now plays your **Sound when you are interrupted** once, so you can judge it with the bar in view. None plays nothing.
- Tips on **Flat fill**, **Class colour on the player bar**, **Own colours on target and focus bars**, **Border takes the cast colour**, **Target and focus bar icon** and the four custom cast bar sizes on the **Cast bars** pages, and on **Flat fill**, **Fill from the right** and **Out of range opacity** of **Swing timers**. Each now says in one line what it does and what depends on it.
- New **Cooldown numbers** on the **Colours and cooldowns** page, just above **Cooldown number size**. It is the game's own **Show numbers on cooldowns**, a game setting the same in every profile. Ticking it puts the seconds left on every action button at once, Flat UI's, the game's and the pet and stance bars, and plays the sample sweep with its numbers. Ticked in a fight, it is written when the fight ends. Before, **Cooldown number size**, **Cooldown number colour** and **Tenths below** changed nothing while the game's option was off, and only a tip sent you to the game's options.
- New **While a proc is up** (on) under **Stay visible** on the **Fading** page, and **Full opacity while a proc is up** (on) on the **Bar block** page. A faded action bar or a faded block now comes back to full in a tenth of a second while a proc alert of the **Trackers** page is up for a spell one of its buttons holds, such as Overpower, Revenge, Execute or a Clearcasting-type proc, whether or not **Glow on the action button** is on. Once the proc ends, it fades again after **Wait before fading**. Before, the art showed beside the character while the button you had to press stayed dim.
- New **Blocks that fade** (**Every block**) under **Fade the block** on the **Bar block** page. **Block 1 only** or **Blocks 2 and 3** fades only those blocks, so the main block can stay solid while a side block of bars 5 to 8 fades, or the other way round. A block left out stays at full, and its bars can be faded on the **Fading** page again. Flat UI's experience bar under block 1 goes with block 1.
- The proc glow on an action button now eases out over about 0.2 seconds when the proc is used or runs out, instead of blinking off in one frame. This covers the soft ring, the game's loop, **Game glow** on Flat UI's own buttons and the 3 second sample glow. A proc that comes back during the fade (Revenge after the next block) brightens it again with no new burst.
- With the options window open and test mode off, the look previews of the action bar pages now bring faded bars and bar blocks to full while they play. These are the sample sweep, the cast fill, interrupt flash and aim samples, the 2 second usability or range tint, the 3 second proc glow sample and the 4 second proc preview of the **Trackers** page. The bars fade again after **Wait before fading**. Before, the previews played on a bar at 30%. In test mode the bars keep fading, so the fade itself can still be judged.
- The page number beside the main bar now answers the mouse like the arrows beside it. Past page 1 it turns from gold to white with a faint square behind it, and it sinks a pixel while you hold the left button. On page 1, where a click does nothing, it stays gold.
- With the mouse over a page arrow or the page number, the mouse wheel now pages the main bar, also in a fight. Up goes to the next page and down to the previous one, with the scroll click and the pop of the number. The tooltips say "Wheel: change page". Away from them the wheel zooms the camera as before.
- A change of **Hotkey position**, **Hotkey offsets**, **Hotkey size**, **Macro name size**, **Macro name position** or **Count position** in a fight, made with the options window open, now says once in chat: "Flat UI: hotkeys, macro names and counts move when the fight ends." Ticking **Page arrows on the main bar** in a fight says "Flat UI: the page arrows show when the fight ends.", or "go when the fight ends." when you clear it. Before, only `/flatui status` said so.
- **Cooldown icons**: the 4 second look preview and test mode's samples now show the tint, keybind and count rows. The second sample is in **Out of range colour** while **Tint when out of range** is on, and the third in **Not enough power colour** while **Tint without enough power** is on. The samples carry keys 1, 2 and 3 while **Keybind on icons** is on, in **Keybind size**. The third shows a count of 5 in **Count colour** at **Count corner** while **Reagent counts** is on. Before, a change of those rows showed nothing on the samples.
- Test mode: a change of **Own usability colours**, **Not enough power colour**, **Cannot use colour**, **Tint icon when out of range** or **Out of range colour** now paints the first sample button in that colour for 2 seconds, as it does with test mode off, and a dragged colour keeps it up. Before, it restarted the sweep on button 4 and never showed the colour.
- **Threat list**: as a tank, **Warn when you lose aggro** now stops pulsing red as soon as your taunt lands and you hold the enemy again, instead of running the full 2 seconds as if the taunt had missed.
- **Threat list**: New **Calm when another tank takes it** (on). With two tanks, an enemy the other tank takes from you (his tank role, the raid's main tank, or in a party his tank stance, form or aura) plays no warning sound, neither bar pulses red, and his bar never turns amber under **Show who is near your aggro** as he builds threat. Anyone else taking it still sets everything off.
- **Healer mana**: in a group where nobody picked the healer role, a Paladin, Druid, Shaman or Priest no longer counts as a healer just by class. You are left out while your talents are in a damage or tank tree or your role reads tank, and anyone in his tank stance, form or aura (Righteous Fury, Bear Form) is left out too. This is read again as talents change and after each fight, so the list, its average and ready are about the real healer.
- **Group tools**: Shift- or Ctrl-right-click on the clear now takes off only your marker or your focus's, as on the markers, instead of wiping every marker in the group. A plain right-click still clears them all, and the tooltip says 'with either button'.
- **Group tools**: in a group where nobody has picked a role, the role poll's tooltip now reads a dim 'Nobody has picked a role yet.' instead of listing everyone in amber under 'No role'. In test mode it lists the sample roles that match its count of 1.
- **Group tools**: at 0 the pull button now flashes and reads 'Go' in green for a moment before it shows **Countdown length** again. Test mode's sample shows Go for one step at each 0.
- **Group tools**: test mode's sample countdown on the pull button now holds still while the test mode bar's **Moving** is off, like the sample tally, and carries on when Moving comes back.
- **Ping bar**: New **Ping**, **Attack**, **Warning**, **On My Way** and **Assist** (on/off, all on) under **Pings** choose which buttons the bar shows. The bar closes up around the rest, and every key under Key Bindings still pings.
- **Party cooldowns**: New **Rows stack** (Down, Up) for the raid group stack. **Up** keeps the bottom of the stack on the bottom of its box in `/flatui move`, your own row still on top, so a stack above your action bars grows upward instead of down over them, as **Bars stack** does for the **Threat list** and **Healer mana**.
- Spell tooltips: **Total** now counts the base of every rank. Before, every rank but the first lost it: Shield Slam Rank 4 read "Total 200 a hit", only its block value, and with the same 101 block value it now reads about 850 a hit, base and block value together. Frostbolt, Flash Heal, Heroic Strike, Mortal Strike, Corruption, Eviscerate, Shield Bash and Revenge count their base again too, and a rank with its own share or ticks, such as Corruption Rank 2, now uses its own.
- The rates under the total are now short dim lines of their own, each with its figure on the right: **Expected** ("645 a hit with crits and misses"), **Per second** ("430 of the global cooldown") and **Per mana** ("2.9, 20 casts from full mana"). Expected was right before: it weighs that same total for misses, dodges, parries and blocks, so it is below the total but for what crits add.
- **Threat lines** now show the threat of one hit or cast as a number, worked out from that same total: Shield Slam Rank 4 in Defensive Stance with Defiance 3/3 now gives about 2,380 a hit. Under it, how that number is made shows once, "(damage + ~255) x 2.16", then each multiplier in force, "Defensive Stance x1.8, Defiance 3/3 x1.2", with a stance now named in full. A heal gives its threat the same way, as "369 a cast, split", and a flat threat its number as before, as Sunder Armor's 552 with "(206 + 5% of attack power) x 2.16" under it. A taunt, and every spell while **With your stats** is off, keep their rule in words.
- The total and threat lines no longer wrap into a block under a spell's tooltip. A long one drops its unit, the multipliers go on as many short lines as they need, and with **Notes under the lines** at **Compact** these lines stay where they are. A spell that hits each target, such as Cleave or Consecration, gives its figures for "a target".
- Bags: an item you place in a section by hand now stays there: every stack of it shows in that section, in the bag and bank windows and for all your characters, until you place it somewhere else, put it back, or delete that section if it is one of your own.
- Bags: a placed item you loot again goes straight to its section instead of New items, and still glows while it is new; it stops being new along with the rest when you close the bags.
- Bags: a placed item that comes into your bags or bank while the window is open, such as from the bank or moved to an empty slot, goes to the end of its section instead of under Added.
- Bags: dropping an item on the section it would go to anyway now keeps it there, so bringing back a deleted section, or changing **Equipment**, **Consumables** or **Trade goods** to a choice that still shows that section, no longer moves it.
- Bags: an item placed in Potions that you drop on Consumables while **Consumables** shows one section stays placed in Potions, so it goes back to Potions when the row splits again.
- Bags: while Junk is not deleted, a grey item you place in a section also goes on your keep list, so none of its stacks turns up in Junk. A placed item you put on your sell list another way shows in Junk until it comes off, then returns to its section.
- Bags: bringing back the items you put back now counts one you have placed in that same section again as back, instead of saying it is in another section.
- Bags: up to 2000 items can be placed by hand (was 500).
- Bags: Place the 7 items that match the search here now also places matching items from New items.
- New **Consumables bar** (off), under **Helpers**: a bar of Flat UI buttons for your consumables. A click uses the item from whichever bag holds it, also in a fight. Each button shows how many you carry across your bags, its cooldown sweep and its tooltip, tinted while you cannot use it now. The first six have keys under **Flat UI** in the game's Key Bindings (**Consumables bar button 1** to **6**), shown short in their corner.
- **Buttons** chooses **Every consumable in your bags**, **Your list** or **Your list, then the rest**. Every consumable in your bags gives one button per item, never from the bank, in the order of the Bags page at Each kind, then by name. The **Kinds in your bags** chips leave kinds such as scrolls out.
- Your list is kept for each character, up to 24 items, and takes any item with a use that you cannot wear, such as the Hearthstone, not only consumables. Drop one on the bar out of a fight: on a button of your list it takes that place, and while **Buttons** shows your list, a + at the end of the bar takes the item you hold. You can also pick one under **Add an item**, or Shift+right-click a button to take it off or put it on. **Take an item off**, **Move an item left** and **Move an item right** do the same from the page.
- An item of your list that you have none of stays on the bar, greyed with a red 0, or leaves a gap (**An item of your list with none left**). A click on it says "Flat UI: no Major Healing Potion in your bags.". With **Every consumable in your bags**, an item you use up leaves the bar once you are out of the fight.
- **Button size**, **Buttons per row**, **Spacing**, **Grow** (right, left, up or down), **Show count** and **Fade out of a fight** with **Faded opacity** set its look. `/flatui move` places it (at first above the chat window, clear of the action bars), and it hides in a pet battle. Test mode shows sample buttons while it is empty. A change in a fight waits for the fight to end, and chat says so once.
- New: **Delete a section** on the Bags page lists every section you can delete now, in the order your bag window shows them. It includes Flat UI's own sections and your own, which are marked (yours) and show how many items are placed in them. Choose one to delete it in both windows and for all your characters. Its items go to the next section that takes them, so nothing is lost, and chat says "Flat UI: deleted Potions from the bags. Deleted sections on the Bags page brings it back."
- While **Equipment**, **Consumables** or **Trade goods** shows its kinds apart, **Delete a section** lists them together, after one line that deletes the whole group at once, such as "Equipment: all 8 kinds". The group's own section, which holds the rest, is marked (the rest). A whole group deleted stays deleted if you change its row later: that line also deletes the group's sections the row does not show now, and **Deleted sections** marks those (not in use now).
- Other is never in the list, since it takes whatever no other section does. New items and Free slots are not either: they are shelves, not sections. A section of your own deleted in **Delete a section** goes as it does from its title's menu, with its items back in their own sections, and **Bring back** in **Custom sections** still restores it.
- Flat UI's bag sections are now deleted rather than hidden. A section title's right-click menu says **Delete this section**, and the Bags page row is **Deleted sections** ("Choose one to bring it back"). The dim title after the last section reads **Deleted (3)** and opens a **Bring back** list. A section that comes back flashes Back, and the tips, chat lines and the Folded sections mark (deleted) use the same words.

## 0.5.41

- For WoW: Forever 1.60.1 build 70938. The interface number stays 16001.
- New: **Reset profile**, **Copy from**, an import with **Replace** and setup's **Finish** are no longer for good until you reload. The **Reset profile** row now has **Bring back** beside **Reset**, and its tip says what it puts back ("Puts Raid back as it was before Reset profile at 21:04: 124 settings, 9 frame positions."). A second press puts back what the first took. After **Delete profile**, the Delete row has **Bring back**, which brings the deleted profile back with the characters that used it and its **Switch automatically** rules. The strip after each says so ("Reset Raid. Bring back on the Profiles page puts it back until you reload."), and Ctrl+Z with nothing to undo adds "Profiles > Bring back puts Raid back as it was before Reset profile."
- A profile picked in a fight now shows on a second line under "Profile: Default" in the title bar, "Raid after the fight" in the accent, so it no longer runs over the **Search** box. A **Switch automatically** switch that waits for the fight's end shows there too, and pointing at it says which. Picking the profile in use cancels either kind ("Stays on Default."). `/flatui profile Raid` in a fight now says "Flat UI: switches to Raid when the fight ends." and switches as the fight ends, with "Flat UI: using Raid."
- Ctrl+Z and Ctrl+Y now show the row they changed: on the tab shown it scrolls into view, its card opens and it flashes in the accent, as a search result does (a **Reset page** undone flashes its first row). A change on another tab is named in the strip: "Undid Border colour: back to #000000 (Unit frames > Bars and pictures)."
- The strip's count now reads "3 changed on this tab. Click to go to each." and lights under the mouse. Each click scrolls to the next changed row, opens its card and flashes it, and goes round to the first again after the last.
- Tabs now show where your changes are: a tab with any setting off its default has a small dot in the accent after its name (**Per frame** counts its eight frames), and a tab under **More** has a dot after its line in the list. The dot goes once the last of those settings is back at its default.
- Home's **Needs a reload** has a small **Undo** on each line, dimmed in a fight, which puts that one change back to its value at login ("Undid Square icons: back to On. It needs no reload now."). You can keep **Side tabs** and drop **Square icons**. The line goes, the notice follows, and Recent changes and Ctrl+Z can make the change again.
- The **Search** box in the options window's title bar has a small cross at its right while you type. A click clears the words, shows the page you were on and keeps the cursor in the box ("Clears the search.").
- Test mode's sample buffs and debuffs now follow **Duration colour** as the real ones do. With **White, red near the end** they turn red under **Red under**, and dragging it repaints them at once (before, they always went red at 10 s). With **As the game has it**, or Minimap and HUD off, they are gold and turn white under 90 seconds, as the game's own times do.
- Every **Reload** (the strip's, Home's, the reload notice's, the welcome card's) now sinks a pixel while you hold it, also where the game's secure /reload button covers it. The reload notice now fades out over 0.2 s on **Later** and when nothing needs a reload any more. **Ignore** and **Turn off and reload** on the taint log box, and **Open Flat UI** on the game's Settings page, now act only when you let go over them. **Open Flat UI** also lights under the mouse.
- The **Export by module** box now says "Press Export chosen, then Ctrl+C" while empty. A click in it selects all of its text again, and Ctrl+C there says "Copied. Paste it where you share it.". The name box of **Import as** shows "Name of the new profile" while empty.
- Heal over time squares: each square read before the pull now goes when its heal over time runs out, also in a fight. A Renew that runs out no longer stays lit while Inspiration or a Prayer of Mending bounce holds the count up, and a Power Word: Shield that runs out no longer wipes the Renew square beside it. A shield used up early still clears the squares the game hides, as before, and a heal over time you recast in the fight loses its square at its old end.
- Heal over time squares on party frames are now at most as tall as the health bar inside its border, so a large **Square size** picked for raid cells no longer hangs over the name row or the power bar. Raid cells keep the full size, and setup's Bex shows the same.
- **Resurrection icon on party frames**: with **Picture** None, as the ready-made **Healer** look sets it, the icon now sits smaller at the left end of the health bar, so the Dead word still reads. It also breathes softly, from full to about half and back every second, while someone resurrects the member, so it is easy to spot beside the raid marker and the debuff icons.
- New **AFK on party frames** (on): a soft grey AFK at the bottom of the picture of a party member who has gone away, or at the left end of the health bar with no picture. It shows as they go and leaves as they come back, also in a fight, and hides while they are dead or offline. The game's frames show none.
- New **Mark the cost of a shift** (on), under **Druid mana in forms**: a thin white line on the druid mana bar at the mana it costs to cast the form you are in again (Cat Form for a cat, Bear or Dire Bear Form for a bear). Fill past the line means a powershift is affordable. Test mode's sample and setup's Cat Form drawing show the line at 35%.
- The druid mana bar's **Mana text** now takes **Power text size**, as the player frame's power text does, and is smaller with no outline under **Smaller, lighter bar text**, live and in setup. At the defaults nothing changes.
- Setup's **Party frames** step now shows Fay lying dead with the resurrection icon on her picture, or at the left end of her bar with **Picture** None. The icon comes and goes as **Resurrection icon on party frames** is ticked, and the row now lives in that step.
- Setup: **Role icons for** now changes the drawings as it changes the game. Tanks and healers takes the damage dealers' icons off the party frames and raid cells, and Healers only takes Mara's tank icon off too.
- README: the test mode list no longer says the pet's target frame has no sample.
- Nameplates: setup's threat samples, the **Threat** drawing and test mode's sample nameplate now use your role's colours and opacity, as live nameplates do. With **Same colours for both roles** or **Same opacity for both roles** off, a damage dealer or healer now sees the damage and healer colours and **Opacity as damage or healer** on them, not the tank's. Picking **Tank** or **Damage or healer** in **Your role** redraws them, and pointing at those rows now marks the sample each one changes.
- **Mongoose Bite** on enemy nameplates now follows the game. Its icon pops over your target's nameplate as Mongoose Bite becomes usable after you dodge, and glows for its 5 seconds (a dodge of yours renews them). It is grey while only mana is short and goes when you cast it or it turns unusable. It moves with you as you change target, since the bite can hit any enemy in melee. An enemy's dodge no longer puts a grey icon you cannot use over it. A warrior's **Overpower** works as before.
- New **Full points stand out** (on) and **Full points colour**: at your most combo points every square turns gold (by default) and the whole row pops once, so a finisher reads at a glance. Spending the points brings the normal colour back. Test mode's sample shows it each time its count reaches 5.
- The preview band above the **Threat** tab of **Nameplates** now draws the threat samples (ring, flash, threat value, the enemy's target and the fades) instead of the plain nameplate with three colour chips. Each threat row brackets the sample it changes.
- Combo points under your target's nameplate now fade in over 0.15 seconds as the first point lands and out over 0.2 seconds as a finisher spends them, instead of blinking on and off. A change of target still shows or hides them at once.
- Kill count: once a kill objective is done, an enemy that comes into view later but is still marked for another quest (an item it drops) now shows the bare quest mark, not a green 15/15 that reads as done. Nameplates that were counting the objective as it finished keep their green count, pop and fade.
- New **Combo point size** (4 to 16, 7 by default) and **Combo points vertical offset** (-20 to 20) on the **Marks and icons** tab resize and move the combo points on live nameplates, in setup's drawing and on test mode's sample. A **Below** threat value moves with them.
- Changing a nameplate size row in a fight (**Health bar height**, **Nameplate cast bar height**, **Bar width**, the level box, **Raid marker size**, the class icon rows) now says once in chat, while the options window shows: "Flat UI: nameplates take their new size when the fight ends." A click-through row says "Flat UI: nameplate clicks change when the fight ends." The change still lands as the fight ends.
- Combo points no longer lie on an enemy player's class icon when **Class icon position** is **Below**. The row hangs just under the icon and moves back under the cast bar on other nameplates.
- **Show health text** has a new choice, **On the target and focus**, which keeps the health number on your focus's nameplate too, as **Show auras** does.
- Cast bars: with Edit Mode's **Lock to player frame** on, **Spell icon on player and pet bars** no longer draws a second icon over the game's own. The game's small icon now gives way to Flat UI's bar-tall one, so only one icon shows on the player and pet bars. Turning the row off brings the game's icon back.
- **Target of enemy casts** now shows the name past the target or focus cast bar ("> Bex") in the class colour of whoever the cast is aimed at: priest white, warrior tan, mage light blue, the way the game's nameplates colour that name. A healer sees at a glance whether the Shadow Bolt is going at the tank or at a cloth wearer. Setup's "> Jon" sample is in warrior tan, and test mode's "> You" is in your own class colour.
- New **Sound when you are interrupted** (None at first) under **Sound** on the **Cast bars** page, with the choices of **No target sound**. It plays as an enemy's Kick, Counterspell, Pummel, Earth Shock or stun stops your cast or channel, so you hear the lockout even while you watch the boss. Moving, jumping or pressing Escape plays nothing. Picking a sound in the options window plays it once.
- New **Share on Low health and Low mana** (on). The lines in the middle of the screen now say how low you are, as "Low health  14%" and "Low mana  8%", and the number follows every change of your health or mana, in a fight too. Setup's **Combat alerts** picture and test mode show "Low health  15%" and "Low mana  12%". Turn the row off for the words alone.
- **Next melee ability names** is now **Spell names**. Its choice **Next melee abilities** (the default) still writes Maul, Heroic Strike, Cleave or Raptor Strike after the number. The new **After every spell** gives every hit of your spells and special attacks its name, as "1,234 Fireball" or "312 Shadow Word: Pain", over enemies and in the Outgoing area. Swings and shots stay bare. Setup's combat text picture writes "Fireball" after its Fire hits while that choice is on.
- New **Latency zone colour** (red at first) under **Latency zone on the player bar**. It recolours the zone at once, with or without **Flat fill**, on the live bar, in setup's cast bar picture and in test mode, so it shows on a class-coloured or red bar.
- **No target** now fades out over a quarter of a second as you take a target, the fight ends or the row goes off, instead of vanishing at once. If it comes back during the fade, it shows whole again at once.
- A cast bar change made in a fight now says, once a fight, which change waits for the fight to end, while the options window shows. Each bar gets its own line ("Flat UI: the focus cast bar takes its new size when the fight ends."), where only the first bar changed got one before. **Interrupt strip height** says "Flat UI: the interrupt strip takes its new height when the fight ends.", and **Target and focus bar icon** says "Flat UI: the target and focus bar icons move when the fight ends."
- **Pushback on your bar**: the "+0.8" now stays on the bar through its finish flash and its Interrupted hold and fades out with the bar, so you can see how far a pushed-back Greater Heal was set back. It clears as the next cast starts.
- **Fade the block** on the **Bar block** page now brings the block's bars, its background panel and Flat UI's experience bar under it back to full in Edit Mode and while `/flatui move` is open, as the **Fading** page's frames already did, so the tabs and the **Action bar block** box no longer sit over bars you can barely see. After **Done** they wait **Wait before fading** and fade again.
- With Flat UI's buttons, pointing at a spell in the spellbook or at a talent now pulses each of Flat UI's buttons that holds it, or its flyout, with the game's gold highlight (flat gold over the icon with **Flat highlights**), in a fight too, as the game does on its own buttons. You can find where a spell sits, or see that it is on no bar.
- **Cooldowns**: out of a fight, with the options window open and test mode off, changing a row of the look now shows every row in the new look for 4 seconds with the samples of test mode: staggered 6 to 15 second sweeps, the first icon ready with its glow, and three sample icons in an empty row, also in a row set to **In combat**. The rows are **Icon size**, **Spacing**, **Icons per row**, **Row direction**, **Extra rows**, **Icon crop**, **Icon border**, **Time left**, **Dim on cooldown**, every row of **States and text**, and the **Position**, **Icon size** and **Row direction** of rows 2 to 4. A dragged slider keeps the samples up until 4 seconds after its last step. Then the real icons come back, with no flash and no ready sound.
- **Glows and animations**: with the options window open and test mode off, a change of **Proc glow** (except **None**) or **Proc border colour** now lights the first sample button's proc glow for 3 seconds in the look chosen: the soft or game glow of **Glow style**, or the border in **Proc border colour**. A dragged colour keeps it up. In test mode, button 2 now also shows the proc glow for 3 seconds after its interrupt flash each round, held still with **Moving** off.
- **Colours and cooldowns**: with the options window open and test mode off, changing **Own usability colours**, **Not enough power colour**, **Cannot use colour**, **Tint icon when out of range** or **Out of range colour** while its row is on now paints the first sample button's icon in that colour for 2 seconds, then gives it back its own colour. A dragged colour keeps it up. With **Grey out icon on cooldown** on, the icon is now grey under the 4 second sample sweep, in test mode too, and changing that row plays the sweep.
- **Bar block**: changing a block's **Bars**, **Add a bar**, **Stack direction**, **Line up bars** or gap in a fight now says "Flat UI: the bar block lays out again when the fight ends." once per fight, and the block lays out as the fight ends. Before, nothing showed until the bars jumped. A /reload or a fade change in a fight says nothing.
- New **Macro name colour** (white by default) and **Macro name position** (**Bottom**, as the game has it, **Top** or **Centre**) on the **Button text** page, for Flat UI's buttons and the restyled game buttons. A long macro name can now sit at the top, clear of a count at the bottom. The colour applies at once, in a fight too; a new position chosen in a fight applies as it ends.
- **Page arrows on the main bar**: a left click on the page number now goes straight back to page 1, in a fight too, with the game's scroll click and the pop. Pointing at it says "Page 4" and "Click: back to page 1"; on page 1 it says only the page, and a click does nothing. Pointing at the number holds a faded main bar or bar block at full, as the arrows do.
- **Background panel** on the **Bar block** page now widens to take in Flat UI's page arrows and page number left of the main bar, with **Background padding** round them, while the block holds the main bar. Before, the arrows hung outside the panel's left edge. The area of **Full opacity under the mouse** matches it.
- **Aim highlight**: after a login or /reload, the first Volley, Flare, Blizzard or Rain of Fire you aim no longer also lights the button of an aspect or aura that is already on, such as Aspect of the Hawk. Only the aimed spell's buttons pulse, from the first aim of the session.
- **Trackers**: adding a tracker (any **Add** row or `/flatui tracker add`), importing from **Paste trackers here**, or **Move up** and **Move down** in **Edit a tracker** now shows the whole row as samples for 4 seconds out of combat, with the new tracker in its place or in the new order. Before, only a chat line said 'Added Arcane Intellect.' Switching a tracker back on, or **Clear own settings**, shows that tracker alone.
- New **Show at a ready check** (on) on the **Trackers** page: for 12 seconds after a ready check, every low or missing tracker shows, also while mounted, in a city or with **Only in combat**. Chat names them once: 'Flat UI: for the ready check, Arcane Intellect is missing and Mana Gem is low.' When everything is fine there is no line.
- **Trackers**: over a sample in test mode, the look preview or `/flatui tracker test`, the tooltip now says what the sample shows ('Sample: Low, 0:18 left, 2 charges', in amber, green or red), with a dim line saying where it comes from. Before, it only said 'A sample of the test.'
- **Trackers**: the look preview now shows the procs of your class too, as their fine sample with their coloured rim, so you see their size and their place at the end of the row.
- **Defensives on you**: changing **Place** out of combat with no defensive on you now shows the three samples where the row now sits for 4 seconds; **Off** shows none. Dragging **Icon size** or **Spacing** no longer flashes the samples white and restarts their sweeps at every step. They flash once as they come, and again only for **Flash when one lands** or a sound pick.
- New **Flash when one lands** (on) on **Your buffs**: each buff gets a quick white flash as it comes, drawn by the game so also in fights, though not when you cast one again while it shows. The samples of `/flatui test icons`, test mode and the look preview flash as they appear.
- **Your buffs**: changing **Place**, **X offset** or **Y offset** out of combat with none of its buffs up now shows the samples where the row now sits, and the samples' countdowns run on through a slider drag. **X offset** now also moves the row where it falls back above the player frame.
- **Absorb shields**: `/flatui absorbs test` in a fight now says 'Absorb shields: the test shows out of combat only.' and changes nothing. It no longer hides the game's shield rows or covers your live total. A look preview sample's tooltip now says 'A sample of the look you are setting.'
- **Cooldown pulse**: a new pick in **Sound of one spell or item** or **Spot of one spell or item** now takes the place of the pulse that shows, with its own icon, name and sound. Before, a second pick, or one right after a look change's test pulse, showed nothing.
- **Cooldown pulse**: with **Pulse spots** at two or three, the test pulses now show a different watched spell at each spot, named 'Spot 2: Frost Nova', so you can tell the spots apart.
- **Group tools**: the small amber number in the role poll's corner now shows only once someone in the group has picked a tank, healer or damage role, so it counts the members still to pick. Before, in a group where nobody had picked one, as most groups on Forever are, it always showed the whole group size ('5', or '40' in a raid). It also turns grey while you cannot start a role poll. Test mode still shows 1 for the sample Kai.
- **Party cooldowns**: when you join a group or /reload, the running cooldowns a member's Flat UI sends you now show their sweep as far along as on his own screen. Before, a Shield Wall with 40 seconds left showed a fresh, full 40 second sweep, and with **Shortest cooldown that sounds** its end could stay silent as if it were a short cooldown. The time left and when it ends do not change.
- New **Shortest cooldown that sounds** on the **Enemy cooldowns** page, under **Ready again** (0 s by default, up to 300 s): a cooldown of your target or focus shorter than this ends without the sound. At 30 s a Kick or a Pummel stays quiet while Divine Shield, Ice Block or a trinket still ring. The flash still shows for every cooldown.
- New **Dim members out of range** (on) on the **Party cooldowns** page: a member's cooldown row fades to 45% while the game says he is out of range, also in a fight, so it matches his party frame dimmed by range. In a raid his name fades with it. A dead or offline member's row still fades to 35%. Test mode shows it on the sample Fay.
- **Healer mana**: test mode now previews Ready to pull. After the sample Druid's 18 second drink, the samples read 96%, 100% and 93% for 4 seconds and the title turns green and reads ready. **Sound when ready** plays once each time test mode comes on. The title now also gives a short soft pulse as it turns ready, in real groups too, so you see the moment without a sound.
- New **Sound when all are ready** (None by default) on the **Group tools** page: it plays once as the last member of a ready check answers ready, with the tick's flash. Test mode's sample tally now reads 4/5 with Eli not ready for 6 seconds, then 5/5 in green with the flash for 4 seconds, and starts again; the sound plays once each time test mode comes on.
- **Threat list**: as a tank, when an enemy turns from you to someone else, the bar of whoever now tanks it pulses red with yours for those 2 seconds, so you see at a glance whom to taunt it off. In test mode the sample Mage's bar pulses with yours at each of his turns.
- **Healer mana**: a title that does not fit the window (a large **Bar height** or a narrow **Bar width**) now reads "Healers 97% · ready", then "97% · ready", instead of cutting off the word ready. The plain average shortens the same way ("Healers 64%", then "64%").
- **Threat list**: with **Pets** off, test mode and `/flatui threat` no longer show the sample Wolf and Voidwalker.
- **Healer mana**: with the test mode bar's **Group** at Raid 10, 20 or 40, the sample now holds eight healers, so you can judge **Number of bars** and **Bars stack** **Up** while placing the list in `/flatui move`. Solo and Party keep the three, and `/flatui healers` is unchanged.
- Bags: with a search typed, a section title's right-click menu now has **Place the 7 items that match the search here**. It places every item the search finds in other sections there at once, so #boe q>=uncommon fills a section of your own such as Auction in one click, in both windows. Chat says "Flat UI: 7 items are placed in Auction.", the section flashes Placed 7, and **Put back the 7 items placed here** undoes it. Items in New items, Junk and Keep in place stay where they are, and nothing is placed in a fight.
- Bags: **Put back the 12 items placed here**, **Empty this section** and the first line of **Items placed by hand** (Put every item back in its own section) can now be undone. Chat adds "Right-click the title to bring them back.", and until the next put back or a reload that title's menu and the first line of **Items placed by hand** offer **Bring back the 12 items you put back**. Each item goes back to its section unless you placed it elsewhere since, chat counts what came back, and the section flashes Back.
- Bags: showing a hidden section from **Hidden (3)** now says so in chat ("Flat UI: Potions shows again.", or "every hidden bag section shows again" for **Show every hidden section**), and the section that comes back flashes Shown. Pointing at **Hidden (3)** now names the hidden sections ("Potions, Rings and Junk"). Neither it nor **+ New section** fades while you search any more.
- Bags: every section of Flat UI's own, such as Trade goods, Quest items or Junk, now has **Colour** in its title's right-click menu, with Plain, Red, Orange, Gold, Green, Teal, Blue and Purple and a tick on the one in use, as your own sections have. The title, its line and its + take the colour in both windows and for all your characters, and Plain takes it away.
- Bags: a bag or quiver in your bags or bank now shows how many slots it has ("16") at its bottom left, in grey so it does not read as an item level. It shows for your other characters too, and goes with **Item level**.
- Bags: the character picker in a window's title now shows each character's gold at the right, as Flat UI last saw it. While a search is typed, that column still counts the matches there.
- Bags: **Split by kind**, **Join into one section** and the Equipment and Consumables choices in a section title's menu, and the window's Sections or Bag by bag button, now show in Home's **Recent changes** with an **Undo**, and Ctrl+Z takes them back.
- Bags: Potions, the name Consumables uses at Each kind, is now one of Flat UI's own section names, so a section of yours cannot take it. Dropping a potion on **+ New section** now suggests the item's own name. Sections you already made keep their names.
- Bags: **Unfold every section** now shows in a title's menu only while a section that window shows is folded. It no longer shows and does nothing when the only folds are of a section you hid or of a kind you no longer split. **Folded sections** marks such a fold (not in use now), as **Hidden sections** does.
- Bags: new search words #placed, for what you placed in a section by hand, and #pinned, for what sits in its pinned slot. Both are in the search box's help, and **Placed by hand** is now one of the quick searches in its right-click menu.
- In `/flatui move`, a press now counts only when you let go over what you pressed, as in the options window since 0.5.39. This covers the toolbar (**Frame list**, **Undo**, **Redo**, **Done**), the grid, **Snap** and **Samples**, a row of the frame list and its eye, **Keep moving**, **Discard** and **Keep** in **Keep your changes?**, and **Pick a frame**, **Reset all**, **Centre ticked** and **Done** on the lower movers bar. Sliding off **Discard** before you let go no longer throws away the session's moves. A right press slid off a box no longer resets the frame or hides it. A toolbar button's label now sinks a pixel while you hold it.
- The experience and reputation bars count a click only when you let go over the bar you pressed. A Ctrl press slid off no longer resets the session count, and a plain press slid off the reputation bar no longer opens your reputations.
- With **Gains over the bar**, a new level now rises as "Level 35" in gold over the middle of the experience bar, a little slower than a gain. A new standing with the same faction rises as "Honored" in the new standing's colour from the end of the reputation fill. Both show with **Animate gains** off too. In test mode every third sample is "Level 35" with the gold flash.
- Reputation: a loss now rises as "-250" in red, and the fill drops at once. Before, losing 250 from Friendly 100 / 6,000 dropped the bar to Neutral 2,850 / 3,000 but floated "+2,750" and slid the fill up with the gain glow. A loss within one standing showed nothing. Gains still rise as "+75".
- When a quest becomes ready to hand in, the part it adds to the **Quests ready to hand in** stretch on the experience bar now lights up in **Quest colour** and fades over about 0.8 seconds, with **Animate gains** on. **Fading**'s **Experience bar** row keeps the bar at full while it glows.
- New **Coordinates on the world map** (on): a small line at the bottom middle of the world map reads "You 45.2, 61.8    Cursor 33.0, 12.5", with **Coordinate decimals**. Your part shows while you are on the map shown and not in an instance. The cursor's part shows while the pointer is over the map.
- **Opacity in combat** under 100%: in a fight, the objective tracker (or Questie's) now eases back to full in 0.2 seconds while the mouse is over it, so you can check 6/8 Murlocs mid-pull. It eases back down as the mouse leaves. The row now has a tip.
- The coordinates, durability and time lines by the minimap, the quest count on the tracker and the experience and reputation bars now show a faint light under the mouse, as the data panel slots do, so you can tell they take clicks.
- New **Zone title size** (100%, 50% to 150%) under **Zone titles and talking head**. It shrinks or grows the big zone name, its PvP line and the subzone name from the next time they fade in. 100% gives the game's size back, and hidden ones stay hidden.
- `/flatui move` has a **Redo** button between **Undo** and **Done**. It puts back what Undo took away, a step (or a group) per click, says how many steps it holds ("Puts back what Undo took away, 2 to redo. Ctrl-Y does the same.") and is dim while there is nothing to redo.
- **Zoom out after** now steps the minimap out one zoom level every 0.08 seconds, so you see it zoom out instead of jumping to the widest in one frame. The mouse coming over the map stops it where it is, and the wait starts again. The row's tip now says what it does.
- **Numbered channels too** (Chat text page) is no longer greyed while **Show recent chat after a reload** is off. Ticked by itself, **Copy recent chat** and `/flatui copy` now hold the lines of General, Trade, LookingForGroup and the other numbered channels.
- Alt-click an item link in chat, as one someone posts in Trade or raid chat, to open the **Gear comparison** window with that item, or bring it up if it is open. In a fight, a window not yet opened this session opens when the fight ends. The window's empty item box now says so.
- In the **Gear comparison** window, Shift-click the item, or the item in **Against**, to put its link in the chat line, in a fight too. Ctrl-click it to try it on. Their tooltips now say how.
- **Copy recent chat** and the web address box now say they took the text when you press Ctrl+C (Cmd+C on a Mac): "Copied 50 lines.", "Copied what you selected." for a part, or "Copied." for an address.
- A web address clicked in a fight now says once a fight that its copy box opens out of a fight, so click it again then. Before, the click did nothing at all.
- **Light the tab that names you**: a lit chat tab now shows, at its top right corner in Your name colour, how many lines named you there since you last looked ("3", up to "9+"). The number goes when you click the tab.
- Test mode's sample buffs and debuffs now turn red at **Red under**, as your real auras do, instead of always at 10 seconds. Moving the slider recolours them within a second.
- The word box of **Other words to colour** no longer keeps its red refusal once you start typing a new word.
- New **Hint while the key is up** (on/off, on by default; Spell tooltips page, under **Show spell lines**). While **Show spell lines** waits for Shift, Ctrl or Alt, a spell Flat UI has lines for ends its tooltip with a dim "Shift: your numbers for this spell" (or Ctrl or Alt). The line goes once you hold the key and the lines come.
- **Item, spell and NPC IDs** is now **Item, spell, quest and NPC IDs**. It also ends a quest link's tooltip with its "Quest ID" and a currency's tooltip with its "Currency ID", following **Show IDs**.
- **Lava tick timer**: when you change one of its rows, the sample bar now shows over the options window for its 4 seconds, where before it sat hidden under the window. It then fades out, and the real bar, `/flatui lava test` and test mode stay under the game's windows as before.
- Data panels: the slot menu now has **Move left** and **Move right**. Each swaps the slot with the nearest slot that shows something and keeps the menu open on the moved slot, so pressing again moves it on. **Add a slot after this one** (or before) now puts the new slot right beside the slot you clicked, not on the first free place at the end of the panel, and its title names where it lands ("Top panel, new third slot"). Each move or add is one line in Recent changes, and one Ctrl+Z undoes it.
- Data panels: while `/flatui move` or Edit Mode is open, the panels come back to full opacity within a tenth of a second, so a panel at a low **Opacity out of combat** or **Opacity in combat** is no longer an empty box while you line it up. They go back to their opacity when you close it.
- Data panels: Escape now closes the slot menu and the **Equipment set** menu, and does nothing else. Before, it closed the options window or opened the game menu and left the slot menu on screen. Every other key still goes to the game, and a menu opened in a fight takes no keys.
- Errands: a `/way` or `/way clear` typed while the world map is open is now kept, not refused. Chat says "Flat UI: the waypoint goes on as you close the map.", and the pin and the navigation arrow appear when you close the map. Typing `/way` again replaces the waiting one, and if the map stays open for a minute it is dropped with one line in chat. `/flatui status` shows a waiting one.
- **Drink and health macros**: with **Say when a macro changes** on, chat now tells you once when you are down to 5 drinks, or to 5 foods while Flat UI Health eats out of combat ("Flat UI: Flat UI Drink has 5 drinks left in your bags."). You see it as you drink or eat after a fight, while there is still time to buy more. It counts every rank your level can use, and says it again only after you restock above 5.
- Data panels: Alt-right-click on a slot that shows another addon's feed now opens Flat UI's slot menu, with that feed ticked. A plain right-click still goes to the feed. When Flat UI draws the feed's tooltip, its last line now reads "Alt-right-click: choose what this slot shows."
- Data panels: Ctrl-click on the **Gold** slot starts this session's gold count again from now, and chat says "Flat UI: the gold count of this session starts again now." Its tooltip's **This session** line and gold an hour, and the bag window's gold tooltip, count from that moment. The tooltip's last line names the click. With **Tooltips** off, Gold still lets clicks through.
- Errands: the **Train all**, **Make room**, **Sell next 12**, **Stack** and **Most** (Buy in bulk) buttons, and **Later** on the spell ranks line, now press like Flat UI's other buttons: while you hold them, the label sinks a pixel and the fill goes back to its resting tone.
- Test mode: with **Moving** on, a shown Gold slot on the data panels now floats a sample gold change every 5 seconds, "+38s 2c" in green and then "-1g 4s" in red. Your real gold and the session count stay as they are.

## 0.5.40

- For WoW: Forever 1.60.1 build 70938. The interface number stays 16001.
- Bags: you can now hide any section of Flat UI's own except New items, Free slots and Other. Right-click its title and choose **Hide this section**. It goes from both windows for all your characters, and its items move to the next section that takes them: a hidden Potions sends potions to Consumables, a hidden Rings sends rings to Equipment, and with **Equipment** at Weapons, armour and jewellery a hidden Weapons sends weapons to Armour. Hiding Junk only moves your junk into other sections; **Sell junk**, the junk coin and your sell list stay as they are, even when you drag junk from one section to another. Hiding Keep in place leaves your pins alone, even when you drop a pinned item on another section, and shows those items by kind. Items you placed by hand in a hidden section return to it when it shows again.
- New: while a section is hidden, a dim **Hidden (3)** shows after the last section of the bag and bank windows. Click it and pick a section to show it again, or pick **Show every hidden section**. A section title's right-click menu also has **Show hidden sections**, and the new **Hidden sections** row on the Bags page lists them all. **Folded sections** and **Items placed by hand** now mark a hidden section with (hidden).
- **Equipment** has a new choice, **Each kind**: Weapons (bows, guns, wands and thrown weapons too), Shields and off-hands, Armour, Rings, Necklaces, Trinkets, Relics, and Shirts and tabards, with Equipment for anything else you can equip.
- **Consumables** has a new choice, **Each kind**: Food and drink, Potions, Elixirs and flasks, Scrolls, Bandages, Enhancements (weapon oils, sharpening stones and the like), and Explosives and devices, with Consumables for the rest. Explosives and devices count as trade goods in the game, but they show here with this choice.
- New **Trade goods** row on the Bags page. **Each kind** shows Cloth, Leather, Metal and stone, Herbs, Elemental, Enchanting, Meat and Parts, with Trade goods for the rest, such as gems. Parts also holds explosives and devices unless **Consumables** is set to Each kind. All three rows stay at One section until you change them.
- The new sections work like the others in both windows: they fold, move, hide, take drops and items placed by hand, end in a + slot, show a count and are found by the search. A section order you saved before puts each new section next to its group. A section title's right-click menu now splits its group by kind or joins it into one: **Split by kind** or **Join into one section** for Trade goods, and the three choices for Equipment and Consumables. The Bags page follows the change.
- Bags: Rings, Herbs, Cloth and the other new section names are now Flat UI's own, so a new section of yours cannot take them. Dropping a herb or cloth on **+ New section** now suggests the item's own name. Sections you already made keep their names and items.

## 0.5.39

- For WoW: Forever 1.60.1 build 70938. The interface number stays 16001.
- A press in the options window, setup, the welcome screen or on the test mode bar now counts only when you let go over what you pressed, as with the game's own buttons. Sliding off before you let go no longer deletes a profile, puts a ready-made look in use, undoes a change or flips a switch. While you hold a button, its label sinks a pixel and its fill goes back to its resting tone.
- **Test mode** asked for in a fight (the switch, its Search result, `/flatui test` or the key) is now kept: chat says "Flat UI: test mode comes on when the fight ends.", the switch shows its knob on, dimmed, while it waits, and test mode turns on by itself as the fight ends if what asked for it is still open. Asking a second time in the same fight takes it back ("Flat UI: test mode stays off."), and so does `/flatui test off`. Before, it only said "test mode after combat" and nothing happened.
- Questions now grow to fit their words, so a long **Delete**, **Copy from**, **Replace** or **Switch automatically** question is no longer cut off; "Enter does not answer this." and the buttons move down with it.
- In setup, Enter now answers a question with its accent button (**Keep choices** when you close setup) and applies the colour picker's colour, as on the options pages. **Start over** is now red with "Enter does not answer this.", like **Delete** and **Reset**.
- Home's **Recent changes** now shows a **Reset page** as one line, "Reset page on Cast bars > Size" with "5 settings", with one **Undo** that puts the whole page back, as Ctrl+Z does; it counts as one of the 50 lines kept. The reload notice's **Undo** is one line too. A click on any **Undo** now says in the strip what it did ("Undid Border colour: back to #000000.").
- New: Ctrl+Y or Ctrl+Shift+Z on any options page puts back what Ctrl+Z or an **Undo** took away, a whole Reset page in one press ("Redid Border colour: #FF3030 again.", or "Nothing to redo."). A new change ends what can be redone. Home's Undo tip now ends with "Ctrl+Y does it again."
- **Profile in use** picked in a fight (on the Profiles page or Home's Profile card) is now kept: the strip says "Switches to Raid when the fight ends.", the title bar reads "Profile: Default, Raid after the fight", and as the fight ends the profile switches and chat says "Flat UI: using Raid." Picking the profile in use again drops the pick, and the pick wins over a waiting **Switch automatically** rule. Before, it only said "Profiles change after combat."
- The **Fonts** page now has a preview band like the module pages: a title, a body line, a number line and small print in the chosen faces, outline, shadow and size beside the game's pop-up text. It follows each change, and pointing at a line finds its row.
- Each empty box of the **Profiles** page now says in dim words what goes in it: "Name of the new profile", "New name for Raid", "Press Export, then Ctrl+C", "Paste a profile here (Ctrl+V)" and "Paste trackers here". A click in an export box selects all of its text again, and Ctrl+C there says "Copied. Paste it where you share it."
- Test mode's sample buffs and debuffs now turn red in their last 10 seconds under **Duration colour** White, red in the last 10 s, in the red the real auras use, and go white again as they loop. Before, they stayed white all the way down.
- Heal over time squares: a buff of yours that comes and goes in a fight, such as Inspiration, a bounce of Prayer of Mending or a Lifebloom ending, no longer wipes the squares read before the pull. They stay while the member has at least as many of your buffs as at that read.
- Heal over time squares on party frames now sit just left of the debuff type icon when **Square corner** is Top right or Bottom right and **Debuff type icon on party frames** is on, so the icon you dispel by is no longer hidden. Setup's Bex and test mode's Eli show them side by side the same way.
- **Squares for your heals over time** now covers a paladin's Sacred Shield, as a gold square. Setup's Bex draws it for a paladin.
- New **Resurrection icon on party frames** (on): the game's resurrection icon on the portrait of a dead party member while someone resurrects them, also in a fight, so two healers no longer resurrect the same one. The game's party frames already show summons.
- Setup's **For healing** card (**Your target and party**) and the ready-made **Healer** look now also turn on **Squares for your heals over time** and **Low health border**, and the stop's party picture shows them as soon as the card is picked.
- **Blink as one lands** now shows in setup: Mara's border and Magic icon blink three times as the drawing comes into view with the row on and each time you turn it on, and test mode's Eli blinks the same way.
- Setup's **Player and target frames** step now draws a druid's player frame as in Cat Form, with the druid mana bar under it at 60%. **Druid mana in forms**, **Mana text** and **Mana bar height** change the picture, and the bar follows **Frame style**, Corners and borders as the live bar does.
- **Pet target frame**: its name and percent now take **Small frame text size** like the pet frame beside it (85% with no outline under **Smaller, lighter bar text**), live, in test mode and in setup.
- **Pet target frame**: Shift-click sets the unit as your focus, also in a fight.
- Turning on **Pet target frame** or changing **Pet target width**, **Druid mana in forms**, **Mana bar height**, **Square corner** or **Square size** in a fight now says once in chat that it happens when the fight ends, while the options window is open.
- New **Combo points on your target's nameplate** (on), on the **Marks and icons** tab of **Nameplates**, with **Combo point colour**: a rogue, or a druid in Cat Form, now sees five small squares just under the cast bar of their target's nameplate. They light in your colour as you build points, the rest stay faint, and the newest pops as it lands, also in a fight. They move as you change target and hide at 0 points or while the game withholds the count. Before, combo points showed only round the target frame's portrait. With **Threat value position** at **Below** the value now hangs under them. Setup's **Marks and icons** drawing shows 3 of 5, and test mode's sample gains one each round.
- Setup's **Nameplate colours and threat** step now draws the threat rows. Beside an enemy, an enemy player, the mouseover and low health, four samples show threat you want (faded to **Opacity of enemies held for you**), changing hands (92%), you do not want (110%, its ring flashing as the drawing opens) and your target with [Gorvak]. Pointing at **Threat glow**, **Flash the ring when threat goes wrong**, the threat value rows, **Name of the enemy's target**, the fade rows and the threat colour rows now marks the sample they change. Before, they were one-line sentences over three colour chips.
- **Show auras**: new choice **On the target and focus**. A mage now keeps their sheep in sight on the focus's nameplate, and a rogue their sap. The threat value, the quest mark and the Overpower icon that stand by the aura rows follow both plates as you change target or focus.
- **Overpower on enemy nameplates** is now **Overpower or Mongoose Bite on enemy nameplates**.
