A hunter who knows Mongoose Bite now gets the same icon over the enemy that dodged them, usable for its 5 seconds, with its glow, swipe, pop and fade. It also shows in setup's **Marks and icons** drawing and on test mode's sample for a hunter.
- New **Overpower icon horizontal offset** and **Overpower icon vertical offset** (-40 to 40, 0 by default), under **Overpower icon size**. They move the icon, and an **Above** quest mark with it, on live nameplates, in setup's drawing and on test mode's sample.
- New **Threat ring thickness** (2 px at first, 1 to 6), under **Flash the ring when threat goes wrong**: the threat ring can now be thicker than a hairline on a 1440p or 4K screen. It grows outward, clear of an elite's border, on live plates, in setup and on test mode's sample.
- **Nameplates**: the threat value and the [Name] of the enemy's target now fade in over 0.15 s as they appear and out as they go, as the threat rings do, so the 85% of a pull no longer flickers on and off over every plate at once. A nameplate that goes still clears them at once.
- **Nameplates**: after you turn the square level box off (**Level box shape** at **Game width**, or **Level box** at **Default**), a sapped, polymorphed or stunned enemy's crowd control icon now sits past the level box again. Before, it lay on the level number until each plate was shown again.
- Test mode's sample nameplate: the kill count now climbs to 8/8 and turns green with its pop, then the mark and count fade just after. The next round brings them back at 3/8 with a fade in and no pop. Before, it counted 3/8 to 7/8 and popped as it dropped back.
- Test mode's sample **Overpower** icon now pops in and fades each round with **Time left on the Overpower icon** off too, as a live icon does; only the swipe goes with that row.
- **Latency zone on the player bar** and **Spell icon on player and pet bars** now work with **Flat fill** off too, on the game's own gold fill. The red zone covers the end of your cast that your connection delay takes (the left end of a channel), and the icon sits left of your cast bar and your pet's, in a fight too and in every Edit Mode layout. Before, both rows were greyed out unless Flat fill was on. Setup's cast bar picture and test mode draw them either way.
- New **Target of enemy casts** (on), on the **Text and icon** tab of **Cast bars**: while the enemy you target or focus casts or channels at someone, "> Bex" shows just past the right end of the target or focus cast bar (past the icon while **Target and focus bar icon** is on the right), so a healer or a tank sees who is about to be hit or sheeped, as nameplates already show. It goes as the cast ends. Setup's cast bar picture and test mode show a sample.
- New **Low health below** and **Low mana below** (20% at first, 5 to 50%), under **Low health and mana warning** on the **You and warnings** tab of **Combat text**. Low health and Low mana now show as soon as your health or mana drops under your own limit, in a fight too, for example Low mana at 35% for a healer or Low health at 30% for a tank. Before, both were fixed at 20%. `/flatui status` names both limits.
- Test mode and `/flatui combat test`: changing **No target colour**, **Size of No target**, **Name the key to target an enemy** or any other **Combat text** row while the sample "No target  (Tab)" shows now repaints it at once in its new colour, size and words. Before, the sample disappeared until the next round. A showing -Combat now also adds or drops its fight length as you turn **Fight length on -Combat** on or off.
- New **Damage on you colour** and **Healing on you colour** (the game's red and green at first) and **Healing on you** (on), under **Your character** on the **You and warnings** tab: they colour the numbers on you in the Incoming area, and **Colour by school of magic** still wins for a spell. Turn **Healing on you** off and the Incoming area shows only what hurts you, so in a raid with five healers the column no longer fills with +numbers. Setup's combat text picture and `/flatui combat test` follow all three.
- The preview band above the **You and warnings** tab now has an "On you" column at the left of the alerts picture: -412 and +300, plus (Dodge) and +20 with **Also misses, dodges and gains**. It follows **Combat text for you**, **Size of your text** and the new colours on you, and pointing at those rows finds it. The picture now draws Low mana only for a class that has mana, as test mode does. Before, a warrior or a rogue saw Low mana there.
- New **Interrupt strip height** (2 px at first, 1 to 6), under the interrupt colours on the **Colours** page: sets how thick the interrupt strip along the bottom of the target and focus cast bars is with **Flat fill** off, so the green or orange is easy to see on a large screen or a tall bar. A change made in a fight applies as the fight ends. Setup's cast bar picture and test mode follow it.
- New **Pushback colour** (red at first), under **Pushback on your bar**: the "+0.5" past your cast bar takes the colour you choose. Each new delay now makes the number pop from a little larger back to its size, so a second hit on your Greater Heal catches the eye. The setup picture and test mode take the colour.
- Changing **Target cast bar width**, **Focus cast bar height** or another cast bar size in a fight, on a bar the game protects then, now says so once per fight in chat while the options window is open: "Flat UI: the target cast bar takes its new size when the fight ends." Before, nothing changed and nothing was said until the fight ended.
- Setup's **Cast bar look** step and the cast bars preview band now draw a **Target, out of range** row (a Frostbolt) in **Interrupt out of range colour**, on the fill with Flat fill and on the strip without, and pointing at **Interrupt out of range colour** finds it. Before, that row bracketed the Focus bar, which is painted in **Interrupt on cooldown colour**.
- Fixed: **Shortest cooldown that flashes** now holds after a stance, form, Stealth or page change and after a reload. A cooldown already running when a button first shows it is measured from when you used it, or by the spell's own cooldown, so a warrior who stance-dances sees Mortal Strike, Overpower and Whirlwind end quietly in every stance while the long cooldowns still flash. Before, any stance change while a short cooldown ran made it flash. Only an item's cooldown already running then still flashes.
- **Proc alerts**: with the options window open, out of a fight and test mode off, changing **Art beside the character**, **Art size**, **Art opacity**, **Art height**, **Glow on the action button**, **Glow style** or **Colour** now shows your class's first proc for about 4 seconds in the new look, its art beside the character and its glow on the button, with no sound. A dragged slider keeps it up until 4 seconds after its last step, and a proc that is really up is just drawn again. Before, nothing showed until a real Overpower, Clearcasting or Execute came.
- New **Sound when ready** (None), under **Flash when ready** on the **Colours and cooldowns** page: Spell alert, Chimes, Bell, Warhorn, Raid warning, Alarm or Whisper warning plays as a cooldown that flashes ends on Flat UI's buttons, at most once a second. With **Shortest cooldown that flashes** at 30 s, Recklessness, Innervate, Evocation or a trinket chimes when ready and 6 second cooldowns stay silent. Picking a sound plays it, and test mode's sample sweep plays it once.
- **Aim highlight** no longer depends on the game's reticle event: Flat UI also asks the game whether a spell waits for its spot or for a click on its target, so Blizzard, Rain of Fire, Flamestrike, Hurricane and Volley pulse either way until they are placed, cast or cancelled. A spell waiting for a click on its target pulses too. A stance, form or aura button already lit stays out of it, and an Auto Shot meanwhile still leaves it on.
- **While casting** on the **Fading** page and **Full opacity as you cast** on the **Bar block** page now hold faded bars at full for 1.5 seconds (a global cooldown) after each instant spell or item use, then **Wait before fading**, so its cooldown sweep can be read. Before, a buff or a trinket outside a fight brought the bars up for about a quarter second, like a glitch.
- **Page arrows on the main bar**: the page number now pops (to about 140%, settling in a quarter second) each time the page changes, in a fight too, so a page changed by Shift and the wheel or a stray key catches the eye. An arrow's tooltip that is up reads the new page at once. Before, it said the old page until the mouse left and came back.
- With **Class colour on the player bar** on, the cast fill on Flat UI's buttons (casts and channels), the **Aim highlight** pulse and the test mode samples now take your class colour, as the player cast bar does, so bar and button agree. Before, a mage's bar filled light blue over a yellow button.
- Test mode's action button samples now work on a character with few spells on the main bar: they take the next shown buttons of bars 2 to 8 that hold something, and with fewer than four in all the sample sweep plays on the last one. Before, a new character or a healer with three spells on bar 1 never saw the sweep, its edge line, numbers or ready flash.
- **Moving** off on the test mode bar now holds Flat UI's action button samples still: button 1's cast fill at about 60%, button 3's aim pulse bright, and the sweep at 2 of its 4 seconds with its **Edge line** and numbers, so **Sweep colour**, **Edge colour** or **Cooldown number size** can be judged. **Moving** on carries on from there. Before, the samples finished and then nothing more played.
- New **Own panel colour** (off), **Panel colour** (near black) and **Panel opacity** (75%), under **Background** on the **Bar block** page. They give **Background panel** its own colour and opacity at once, so it can be fainter than the frames or match the action bars' **Bar background**. The panel still fades with the block. Off, it keeps the Flat UI page's background colour and opacity as before.
- **Trackers**: out of combat, changing a row of **Look and position** (**Shape**, **Icon size**, **Bar width**, **Bar height**, **Bar fill**, **Colour**, **Bar texture**, **Icon border**, **Charges**, **Name**, **Time left**, **Countdown number size**, **Row direction**, **Spacing**, **Close up the gaps**, **Place**, the side or the offsets) now shows every tracker that is on in the new look, at full opacity, as its sample with 20 charges and 10:00. They stay until 4 seconds after your last change, so a slider drag keeps them up, then fade back to what each tracker really shows. **When missing** shows the missing sample: red, pulsing or with the word. In **Edit a tracker**, a change of Shape, Icon size, Colour, Place, X, Y or When missing shows that one tracker the same way. Before, a tracker set to Only when low or missing showed nothing unless test mode was on.
- **Trackers**: a tracker you fix, by casting Arcane Intellect, restocking arrows or putting a poison back on, now flashes green for a moment and stays at full opacity that long before it fades. With **Show** at Always it flashes and stays. This works wherever Flat UI reads the fix itself: out of combat, and in fights for weapon enchants, items and ammunition.
- **Defensives on you** and **Your buffs**: the time left on an icon and its charges now grow and shrink with **Icon size**. They keep today's size at the default (32 on Defensives on you, 40 on Your buffs), so '10' no longer spills over a 16 px icon and a 64 px icon no longer has a tiny number. This covers the game's icons in fights, the out-of-combat icons and the samples. Time left **Under the icon** keeps its size.
- New **Sound when one ends** (None by default; Raid warning, Alarm or Whisper warning) on **Defensives on you**: plays once as a listed defensive from someone else, such as Blessing of Protection or Pain Suppression, leaves you. The game plays it, so it works in fights too. Spells you know yourself are left out. `/flatui test icons` plays it as its samples go.
- **Defensives on you**: picking **Sound when one lands** or **Sound when one ends** out of combat, with no defensive on you, now plays the sound and shows the three samples flashing in as they land, for about 4 seconds.
- **Defensives on you**: the chat line at the end of `/flatui test icons` now reads 'Defensives on you: the sweep ended on 3 of 3 icons. Sound when one lands: Raid warning, played.' (or None, or that the game played no sound). Before, it said 'Sound: played' whatever the row was set to, because it took the sound of the other rows' samples.
- **Defensives on you**: the 4-second look preview no longer draws its samples over a real defensive the game shows, including an external that is not on the list. Out of combat where the game makes no icon row, the icons are read again as a test or preview ends. Before, a defensive that landed or ended during those seconds showed wrong until the next buff change.
- **Absorb shields**: `/flatui absorbs test` and the look preview now include your target's sample bar (640 of 2400) while **Your target's total** is on. Before, that bar froze at its last value during the samples. Ticking **Your target's total** out of combat now shows the samples for a few seconds, so you see where the target's bar sits with nothing targeted.
- **Absorb shields**: the time left on a shield's bar, such as Power Word: Shield or Ice Barrier, now turns amber in its last 5 seconds, as on **Your buffs** and **Defensives on you**. This covers Flat UI's bars, the samples and the game's rows in fights. Before, it counted down to 0.0 in white.
- **Cooldown pulse**: an item picked in **Sound of one spell or item** or **Spot of one spell or item** now previews with its name in its quality colour (green, blue or purple), as its real pulse shows it. Before, the preview's name was white. Spells stay white.
- New **Show who is near your aggro** (on), on the **Threat list** page under **Warning**: while you are a tank, the bar of any member at **Warn at** or above who is not tanking turns a steady amber, with no sound, so you see the Mage at 96% before his next crit and can taunt or slow down. **Warn at** now stays live with **Warn near aggro** off, since tanks read it too. In test mode, as a tank, the sample Rogue climbs to 94% and turns amber between the Mage's turns.
- New **Ready at** (90% at first; 0 means never) and **Sound when ready** (None by default), in a new **Ready to pull** section of **Healer mana**. Out of combat, once every healer shown has at least Ready at of their mana and none is dead or offline, the title turns green and reads "Healer mana 97% · ready", and the sound plays once, so a tank can pull without watching the bars. It rings again only after a healer was seen under Ready at, and stays quiet at login, on zoning and in a fight.
- **Healer mana**: `/flatui healers` in a fight now says "Healer mana: the sample shows out of combat." Before, it replaced the real healer bars with the samples for 8 seconds just when they mattered. In test mode the sample Druid's drink now counts down from 18s to 1s and starts again; before, it always read 18s.
- New **Shortest cooldown that sounds** (0 s at first, up to 300), under **Sound** on **Party cooldowns**: a cooldown shorter than it ends without the sound. At 60 s a rogue's Kick or a warrior's Pummel stays quiet while Shield Wall, Blessing of Protection or Innervate still ring. 0 sounds for every cooldown, as before.
- `/flatui test icons` now follows **Flash when ready** and **Sound** of **Party cooldowns** and **Enemy cooldowns**. Before, every sample flashed and played Spell alert even with Flash off and Sound None. In test mode the **Enemy cooldowns** target and focus bars now play its **Sound** once, as **Party cooldowns** has done since 0.5.38.
- **Group tools** raid markers: Shift-click now puts a marker on yourself and Ctrl-click on your focus, in a fight too, through the game's own secure marker action. Shift- or Ctrl-right-click takes theirs off, and Shift- or Ctrl-click on the clear does the same for you or your focus. The tooltips say so, and a marker greyed for no target says Shift-click still marks you.
- **Group tools** role poll: a small amber number in the icon's corner now counts the members with no role ("2"), like the ready check's 3/5, and goes away at 0. In a party where someone has a role, the tooltip adds "No tank" or "No healer" in amber when nobody holds that role. Test mode shows 1 for the sample Kai.
- **Group tools** tooltips now write each name in its class colour: the role poll's "Tank: Mara" and "Damage: Ivo, Fay", and the ready check's "Not ready" and "No answer" lists. A healer finds the priest who is not ready at a glance. The labels keep their dim or amber colour.
- **Group tools** pull countdown: the last three seconds are now red, and at 0 the button gives the white flash of cooldown icons; test mode's looping sample flashes at each 0. The ready tick now flashes the moment the last member answers ready. Out of combat, the mouse wheel over the pull button makes **Countdown length** a second longer or shorter (3 to 60), and its number and tooltip follow at once.
- **Bags**: a section's title now takes the same drops as its + slot. Drag an item from your bank, or a worn piece, onto a title such as Trade goods and it moves into a free slot of your bags and shows in that section ("Moved in: Runecloth"); a merchant's item dropped there is bought into a free slot ("Bought: ..."). A refusal says why in chat. Before, the title flashed "Kept:" and the item went back to the bank.
- **Bags**: with a merchant's item, a spell or a macro on your cursor, a click on a section's title no longer folds it, Shift-click no longer folds the others, right-click no longer opens its menu, and **+ New section** no longer opens the name box. A merchant's item, an item in the trade window or a bag from the bag bar dropped on + New section is now refused with a line, instead of making a section for an item that is not in your bags.
- **Bags**: + slots, the gaps between a section's slots and section titles now light only for an item that section would take. Hold a quest item and Junk stays unlit, its tooltip saying "Quest items are never sold." in red; nothing lights in a fight. Swapping the item on your cursor with a click on another slot now lights the sections for the new item.
- **Bags**: picking up an item at a merchant now lights every + slot and title that would buy it at once, and letting go among a section's slots buys it into a free slot there, as for an item from the bank.
- Alt+right-click an item in Flat UI's bag window to put it on your sell list: the junk coin appears, Junk flashes "On your sell list" and Sell junk sells it next time. Alt+right-click it again to take it off; a grey item then goes on your keep list. Quest items and items with no sell price are refused, a pinned item says to unpin it first, and nothing changes in a fight (**Junk coin** tip).
- **Delete this section** in a title's menu, and **Delete** in + New section's menu, are now red and say what goes ("Delete this section (12 items go back)"). Until the next delete or a reload, + New section's right-click menu and **Custom sections** on the Bags page start with "Bring back Raid (12 items)", which restores its name, its items, its fold, its colour and its place, and the section flashes "Back". An item you placed elsewhere since stays where you put it.
- Right-click a section of your own and pick a colour under **Colour** (Plain, Red, Orange, Gold, Green, Teal, Blue or Purple). Its title takes that colour in both windows and for all your characters, and its + slot's + shows it; the count stays dim.
- Right-click a section's title for **Put back the 3 items placed here** (**Empty this section (3 items)** on a section of your own): they go back to their own sections, chat says "3 items go back to their own sections." and the window lays itself out again. The tooltip of one of Flat UI's own sections now says how many items are placed there by hand.
- An item's tooltip in Flat UI's bag and bank windows now ends with a dim "Placed by hand in Raid" when you placed it in its section, or "Pinned to this slot. Ctrl+right-click unpins it." for a pinned item, whether or not **Counts in tooltips** is on.
- Dropping an item on **+ New section** now opens the name box with a name already typed and selected: the item's kind, such as Herb or Cloth, else its type, else its name. Enter makes the section at once, and typing replaces the name.
- The bank window's bank bag slots: a right-click with an empty cursor now does nothing (it showed only that bag, as a left click does), a bag you lift turns its slot grey until you put it down, and every bought slot lights while a bag is on your cursor, as the bag window's **Bag slots** row does.
- `/flatui move` typed in a fight after "Movers pause for combat." now closes them for good: chat says "Flat UI: /flatui move stays closed after the fight." and they no longer open when the fight ends. Before, they opened anyway.
- `/flatui grab` in a fight now says "Flat UI: /flatui grab starts when the fight ends." When the fight ends the movers open with the pick running. A second grab in the same fight takes it back. Before, the pick was lost.
- `/flatui move`: Ctrl-Y, or Ctrl-Shift-Z, puts back what **Undo** just took away, one step at a time, with a **Space across** or **Space down** counted as one step. A new move clears what can be redone. The Undo tooltip shows how many steps can be redone ("Ctrl-Y puts it back again, 2 to redo.").
- `/flatui move` toolbar: **Snap** and **Samples** now click like the game's checkboxes and their knobs slide, as the other switches do. Both switches and the grid's **Off**, **Dim** and **Bright** light up under the mouse, as on the test mode bar.
- Experience bar tooltip: a new line under the first says what is left, "To level: 14,850 (55%)". The reputation tooltip now reads "4,200 / 12,000 (35%)" and "To Revered: 7,800" ("To the next standing" for a friendship, renown or paragon faction), as the data panels do. Neither line shows at the top level or the top standing.
- **Gains over the bar**: the "+1,240" now always draws over the action buttons and over the other bar, where before it could hide behind them. With **Bar text place** at **Above the bar** it starts just over the bar text instead of on top of it.
- Test mode: while **Moving** is on, every 4 seconds each shown experience or reputation bar plays a sample gain. "+1,240" rises from the experience fill and "+75", in the standing's colour, from the reputation fill, with the glow of **Animate gains**. Your real numbers and the session count are not touched.
- **Fading**: every faded frame now comes back to full while `/flatui move` is open, as it already did in Edit Mode, so a frame at 0% is not an empty box while you line it up. After **Done** it waits **Wait before fading** and fades again.
- **Fading**: the **Experience bar** row now holds Flat UI's bar at full while a gain rises over it, its fill slides up or a new level or standing flashes, then waits **Wait before fading** and fades again.
- New **Wrong stance or form** (on/off, off by default) under **Hide error messages**. It hides "Must be in Battle Stance", "Can't use items while shapeshifted" and the like, with their sound. An NPC that will not talk to you while shapeshifted still says so.
- The experience bar now reads the game's bar text lock from a field instead of calling one of the game's Lua methods. The bar text shows and hides as before.
- A click on the map text in a fight now says "Flat UI: the world map opens when the fight ends.", as the data panels do.
- Trinket queue window: with the chat box open, Shift-clicking a trinket's icon, the worn trinket's icon or a trinket under **In your bags** now puts its link in the chat line in a fight too. Before, in a fight the same click moved a trinket to the top of the list, printed "trinkets change after the fight", or did nothing.
- New **Light the tab that names you** (on), under **Mentions** on the **Chat text** page: when a line names you (or one of your **Other words to colour**) in a chat window whose tab is not chosen, such as Trade, Guild or LFG, that tab's box and label take Your name colour with a slow, soft pulse until you click the tab. It needs **Chat tabs**.
- The **Trinket queue** window no longer opens on top of the **Gear comparison** window. Until you give it a place of its own, it opens right of the Gear comparison window while that is open, or right of the item finder docked there. On a screen with no room there, it opens 28 px down and right so both title bars show. In `/flatui move` its box now starts beside the Gear comparison box instead of inside it.
- **Other words to colour**: the row's first line is now **Add a word...**, which opens a small box: type the word and press Enter. A word it cannot take (too short, has a space, already coloured, 20 already) says why in red in the box, and Escape closes it. `/flatui words remove tank` before any word was added now says the word is not in the list.
- New **Copy recent chat**, on the **Chat text** page, and `/flatui copy`: they open a tall box with the last 50 or 200 lines Flat UI heard: say, yell, emotes, party, raid, guild, officer and whispers, plus the numbered channels with **Numbered channels too**. With **Show recent chat after a reload** on, lines from before the reload are included too. Lines read as "[21:04] [Party] [Mara]: text", links show as their name in brackets and colour codes are left out. Everything is selected for Ctrl+C, Escape closes the box, and it never opens in a fight. `/flatui copy 200` takes up to 200 lines.
- Trinket queue window: **Copy the Top trinket list** (or Bottom) now says in the window's foot what it did, as "Took 5 from the Top trinket list, with each Delay and Stop.", and **Queue now** says "Queue now: the Top trinket list looks again now." (in a fight: "...as the fight ends."), each for 8 seconds. In a fight, Shift-clicking a slot's square on the Character window now says the queue looks again as the fight ends. The Copy tooltip names the exact command, as "/flatui queue copy top does the same".
- **Item, spell and NPC IDs** (was **Item and spell IDs**): a creature's or vehicle's tooltip now ends with a grey "NPC ID: 448" line, following **Show IDs**. Players get none.
- New **Red under** (10 s by default, 3 to 60), under **Duration colour** on the **Buffs and debuffs** page, shown at **White, red near the end** (was **White, red in the last 10 s**): it sets when the time on a buff, debuff or totem turns red, for example 60 s for a flask or 3 s for a Sunder. Saved settings do not change.
- Item finder: the search box now has a small cross at its right while a search is typed, which clears it and searches again. Up and Down bring back the last five searches of the session, newest first, and Down goes forward again to what you typed, as in the bag search box.
- **Sound when you are named** and **Sound on a swap** now play a picked sound once, through the options window alone. Undoing a change of either with Ctrl+Z or Home's **Undo** no longer plays a sound.
- **Data panels**: the right-click menu of a slot now dims what shows nothing for this character and says why, as "Soul shards (only for a warlock)", "Ammunition (no bow, crossbow, gun or thrown weapon)" or "Experience (at the top level or with experience off)". You can still pick one. A pick that leaves the slot with no room now says so in chat: "Flat UI: Soul shards shows nothing for this character now (only for a warlock), so the third slot of the top panel takes no room." Picking None does the same. If the panel has nothing left to show, the line adds that the panel hides until a slot has something to show. Before, the slot just vanished.
- **Data panels**: while a panel has a slot set to None, the right-click menu now has **Add a slot after this one** above **Data panels options**. It opens a second menu in the same place, titled "Top panel, new fifth slot", listing every kind but None. A pick fills that slot and the panel grows at once. Ctrl+Z and Home's **Recent changes** undo it, as they do any slot pick.
- **Data panels**: a long right-click menu, from many LibDataBroker feeds or **Text size** at 150 to 200%, now continues in a second (and third) column beside the first, each as tall as the screen allows, with a thin line between them. The last line is still **Data panels options**, and every line can be clicked. Before, the lines past the screen edge were cut off, often including Data panels options.
- **Data panels**: each change of your gold now floats from the **Gold** slot, as "+38s 2c" in green or "-1g 4s" in red. It rises about 10 px and fades over about 1.2 seconds, as in the bag footer since 0.5.36. A quick run of changes, such as a Sell grey items pass or looting a pack, adds up to one growing sum. On a panel in the upper half of the screen it drops below the slot instead, so it never runs off the screen. Nothing floats at login, after a loading screen or in test mode.
- **Data panels**: with **Opacity in combat** or **Opacity out of combat** below 100%, a panel now rises to full opacity in a tenth of a second under the mouse and settles back over 0.3 seconds as the mouse leaves. A fight eases it to Opacity in combat the same way. Before, it snapped. A panel that appears still takes its opacity at once.
- **Data panels**: the right-click menu now stays open when the panels refresh under it, for example when an addon or a feed loads, the UI scale changes or a loading screen passes. It is drawn again with its tick. Before, it closed by itself.
- **Data panels**: in a fight, clicking the minimap coordinates and then the **Coordinates** slot now prints one line, "Flat UI: the map opens when the fight ends.", instead of two.
- **Data panels**: changing **Ammunition minimum** or **Thrown weapon minimum** on the Trackers page now recolours the **Ammunition** slot at once. Before, it kept its old gold or white until the next bag update.
- **Mount key**: in the box of **Press a key to set it**, a turn of the mouse wheel that zooms your camera is now asked about first, like a key that moves you: "Mouse Wheel Down zooms the camera out. Turn it again to use it anyway, or press another key." Before, one scroll over the box made the wheel the Mount key at once, and your zoom was gone. A second notch of the same scroll, or a held key's repeat, within 0.4 seconds no longer counts as the second press. The box also shows the key in use now on a dim line, as "Now: Shift-Z".
- **Lava tick timer**: out of a fight, with the options window open, changing **Lava bar width**, **Lava bar height**, **Jump lead**, **Flash when it is time to jump**, **Lava bar colour**, **Jump colour** or **Jump sound**, or picking **In lava zones** or **Anywhere**, now shows the sample bar in the new look for about 4 seconds. The bar is silent and fades out over 0.4 seconds. A slider drag keeps it up until 4 seconds after its last step. Before, you saw nothing without `/flatui lava test` or test mode.
- **Cursor ring**: with **In combat only** or **When you shake the mouse**, changing **Cursor ring size** or **Cursor ring colour**, or picking either choice, now shows the ring whole around the pointer for about 1.2 seconds and then fades it out. This works out of a fight with the options window open. A slider drag keeps it up until 1.2 seconds after its last step. Before, the ring stayed hidden and the change could not be seen.
- **Release at once in battlegrounds** now also releases your spirit after a /reload or a relog while you are dead in a battleground, a moment after the loading screen, with the usual "Flat UI: Released your spirit (battleground)." Before, Flat UI left you lying there.

## 0.5.38

- For WoW: Forever 1.60.1 build 70938. The interface number stays 16001.
- The options window's title bar now names the profile your changes go into, as "Profile: Raid", dim, left of the **Test mode** switch on every page. Point at it to see how many characters use that profile, and click it to open the Profiles page. It lights in the accent for a second when the profile changes while the window is open, for example when **Switch automatically** moves you to Raid.
- **Custom scale** is now greyed and reads "not used now" while **Pixel-perfect scale** or **Scale below 65%** sets the scale, and **Small scale** does the same while Pixel-perfect scale is on; each row's tip names the row that sets the scale. In setup's **Scale** step, **Scale below 65%** and **Small scale** are now main rows, so the slider that sets the scale always shows. Before, Custom scale looked live at the defaults but changed nothing.
- **Recent changes** and Ctrl+Z: renaming the profile in use now keeps its recent changes. A change that **Reset profile**, **Copy from**, an import that replaces or setup's Finish took away now leaves the list, and Ctrl+Z skips it, until its value comes back. Before, Ctrl+Z could write a value from before an old change into a freshly reset profile.
- Ctrl+Z after **Reset page** now puts the whole page back in one press, rows changed earlier in the session included: "Undid Reset page on Cast bars > Size: 5 settings back." The reset now says "Reset 5 settings on Cast bars > Size. Ctrl+Z puts them back." Each line on Home still has its own **Undo**.
- Setup no longer keeps your character walking after you let go of W when you pressed Enter, Left or Right on a stop, or Escape to close a list or the colour picker meanwhile, as on the options pages since 0.5.34.
- Messages in the options window's bottom strip, such as "Undid Border colour: back to #000000." or "Press Ctrl+C to copy.", now fade in and stay for about six seconds whatever the mouse crosses, then the strip shows the count of changes on the tab again. Before, they went as soon as the mouse crossed a row, or stayed for minutes.
- Questions now fade in. The button Enter presses is in the accent. On a question Enter does not answer (**Delete**, **Reset**, **Copy** and **Replace**), that button is red, with "Enter does not answer this." under the question.
- The welcome screen and the **Your screen is set** card now fade in with the game's window sounds, as the options window does. You can drag them by the title row while they are open, and a new **Close** at the top right closes them without choosing anything, as Escape does.
- The options window's **Reload** now reads "Reload (2)" in the accent while changes wait for a reload, and pointing at it lists them as the reload notice does ("Square icons: Off now, On at login"). With nothing waiting it is plain, and its tip says nothing needs a reload.
- Every switch now clicks with the game's own checkbox sound, higher for on and lower for off, and its knob slides across. That covers the setting rows, a module's switch in the header and on Home's tiles, the **Test mode** switch and **Moving** on the test mode bar. A switch that changes by itself, after a profile switch, Reset page or Ctrl+Z, slides without a sound.
- New **Blink as one lands** (on), under **Party and raid frames** on **Unit frames**: as a party or raid member gets a debuff you can dispel, the border inside their health bar blinks three times over about 0.6 seconds, with its type icon on party frames, also in a fight, then stays. It plays at the moment **Sound for a debuff you can dispel** would. It never plays on the first read of a member, so nothing blinks at login, on zoning or on joining a group.
- **Squares for your heals over time**: in a fight, the squares read before the pull now stay while the member has as many of your buffs as before. A Sunder, a debuff or another healer's buff no longer wipes them until the fight ends. When one of your buffs ends, the game does not say which, so the squares it hides go and only those it shows plainly stay. A heal over time cast in the fight still shows once the fight ends.
- New **Enemies below** (20% at first, 5 to 50%), under **Low health border**: the limit of the low health border on a target, focus, boss or target of target you can attack, for Execute at 20% or Hammer of Wrath. A unit you can assist, such as the tank a healer targets, keeps **Low health below**. Setup's Defias Pillager follows the new row.
- **Also on target, focus, boss and target of target frames** (was **Also on target, focus and boss frames**): the target of target and focus target frames now take the low health border too. A healer who targets the boss sees the tank drop on the target of target frame, and the border beats with every other border.
- New **Mana text** (Percent by default; Current or None), under **Druid mana in forms** on the **Player frame** tab: a feral or bear druid now sees their mana as "34%" or the amount just right of the thin mana bar, in the bar text font. It fades in and out with the bar. Test mode's sample reads 60%.
- New **Mana bar height** (4 px at first, 2 to 12): sets the height of the druid mana bar, changed out of combat.
- Setup's **Focus, pet and target of target frames** step now draws the pet target frame right of the Wolf pet while **Pet target frame** is on: Training Dummy at 65% with a Skull left of it, as wide as **Pet target width**, in the unit frames' border, background and corners, with the amber border while **Border while it is not your target** is on. Before, these four rows were only sentences.
- As you level, the new level on the player frame (in the name row, or on the portrait with **Level position** On the portrait) now grows to almost twice its size and settles back over half a second, also in a fight, as the experience bar lights in gold.
- **Pet target frame**: as the frame appears, its background, border and amber border now fade in together with the bar and its words. Before, the dark box and its border popped on at full strength while only the bar faded in inside them.
- Setup's **Party frames** step: Bex now shows the heal over time squares of your class in their real colours and places, for example green, white and yellow for a priest, or blue and orange for a shaman. A class without heals over time keeps the two sample squares.
- Setup now draws **Raid marker on party frames** (a Star on Mara in **Auras, flags and icons**). In **Boss and raid frames** it draws **Raid marker on raid frames** (a Skull on Aria's raid cell), **Leader and assistant icons on raid frames** (Aria's leader crown, Dru's assistant icon), **Border for your target** and **Target border colour** (Cole drawn as your target, in the game's white or your colour). Before, these rows were only sentences.
- **Kill count beside the quest mark** now ends on a finished count. As your last kill of an objective counts, the marks of the other enemies of that kind turn to 8/8 in green and pop. The mark then fades just after the pop settles, in a fight too. Before, they faded away still reading 7/8, or the count blinked off first. A mark the game still shows for another part of the quest keeps its green 8/8.
- New **Quest mark horizontal offset** and **Quest mark vertical offset** (-40 to 40, 0 by default), under **Quest mark position** on the **Marks and icons** tab of **Nameplates**. They move the quest mark and its kill count together, on live nameplates, in setup's **Marks and icons** drawing and on test mode's sample.
- **Nameplates**: with **Level box** at **Hidden**, the threat value at **Right**, the [Name] of the enemy's target, a **Right** quest mark and a sapped or polymorphed enemy's crowd control icon now sit right at the end of the bar again. Before, the game's crowd control row still kept room for the invisible level box, so they floated about a box width past the bar.
- **Threat value position** **Above** now puts the value just over the enemy's debuffs, and it rises as their row wraps. An **Above** quest mark and the **Overpower** icon now stand over the value. Before, the 85% lay on your Rend and Sunder icons, and an Above quest mark sat on the number. Test mode's sample shows the same order.
- **Nameplates**: an **Above** quest mark now stands just over the **Overpower** icon while the icon shows, and drops back once it has faded. Before, for 5 seconds after a dodge, the icon covered the mark and its 3/8.
- **Show auras** at **Only on the target**: on the plates whose auras are hidden, an **Above** quest mark, the **Overpower** icon and an **Above** threat value now sit over the name, and they move as you change target. Before, they hung high over the invisible debuffs.
- **Show auras** and **Show health text** at **Only on the target** now find your target by its nameplate. On a map where the game withholds which plate is your target, your target now still shows its auras and health text.
- **Nameplates**: the **Overpower** icon now gives a small pop, from 115% back to its size over 0.15 s, each time a new dodge renews its 5 seconds. Before, only the swipe started over.
- **Nameplates**: a threat ring now fades in over 0.15 s as an enemy starts to need you and fades out as it settles, so the rings no longer blink in an 8-mob pull. A ring turning to You do not want keeps its double flash with **Flash the ring when threat goes wrong**. A plate that leaves and a change of the options still clear the ring at once.
- Test mode's sample nameplate now moves as live plates do. It eases to each round's opacity over 0.2 s, and its threat ring eases in and out. A warrior's sample **Overpower** icon pops in as each round starts and fades over the round's last quarter second. All of it holds still with **Moving** off.
- **Pushback on your bar** no longer sits on top of the game's own seconds: while Edit Mode's Show Cast Time shows them and **Cast time on your bars** is off, the red pushback now comes right after them ("1.3  +0.5"). It stays at the bar's end otherwise.
- **Pushback on your bar** now keeps its total until its own cast ends. A stop or interrupt of another spell (one you queue or press during the cast) no longer wipes it or loses the next delay.
- The five **Combat text** tabs of the options window now show a preview band above their rows, as the 0.5.35 notes said: the numbers over an enemy and friendly units and the Incoming and Outgoing columns, following each change. Pointing at a number finds its row. **You and warnings** shows the combat alerts.
- New **Only in a fight** (on) under **Low health and mana warning**: Low health and Low mana now show only in a fight, so eating or drinking at low health or mana after a pull keeps the middle of the screen clear. Turn it off to see them out of a fight too, as before.
- New **Low mana colour** (blue): Low mana no longer takes **Low health colour**, so the two lines read apart at a glance. Test mode now shows Low mana under the sample Low health for a mana user.
- New setup step **Combat alerts**: a picture draws "+Combat", "No target  (Tab)", "Low health", "Low mana" and "-Combat  1:24" in their colours, sizes and font, each while its row is on. It also shows above the **You and warnings** tab. `/flatui combat test` now plays one quiet round of the alerts, two seconds apart.
- New **Total cast time too** (off) on the **Text and icon** tab of **Cast bars**: the time on your bars, your pet's and the target and focus bars reads the seconds left and the whole cast, as "1.2 / 2.5". Setup's cast bar picture and test mode show it too.
- **Colour by school of magic** now also colours spells that hit you in the Incoming area: a Frostbolt shows light blue and a Fireball orange. Physical hits stay red, and a spell of several schools keeps the game's purple.
- **Interrupt readiness on enemy casts** now shows on the target and focus cast bars without **Flat fill**, which is off by default. A thin strip along their bottom takes **Interrupt ready colour**, **Interrupt on cooldown colour** or **Interrupt out of range colour** while the enemy casts something you can interrupt. With **Flat fill** on the whole fill is coloured as before. Setup's cast bar picture draws the strip, and the four interrupt rows moved to the **Cast bar look** step.
- The **Combat text: alerts** box of `/flatui move` now reaches from +Combat down to Low mana, so you can see where the low lines fall. Spots you saved stay where they are.
- Test mode now shows Flat UI's action buttons at work. Every 5 seconds, the first four shown buttons of the main bar that hold something play a sample: button 1 fills as a 2 second cast in the cast bar's **Cast colour** and lands, button 2 flashes as an interrupt, button 3 pulses as an aim, and button 4 runs a 4 second sweep in **Sweep colour** and **Sweep opacity**, with its **Edge line** and numbers, ending with **Flash when ready** in **Flash colour**. Each plays only while its row is on. Before, test mode showed nothing on the action bars.
- **Colours and cooldowns** and **Glows and animations**: with the options window open and test mode off, changing **Cooldown sweep**, **Own sweep opacity**, **Sweep colour**, **Sweep opacity**, **Edge line**, **Edge colour**, **Cooldown number size**, **Cooldown number colour**, **Tenths below**, **Flash when ready** or **Flash colour** plays a sample sweep in the new look on the first button of Flat UI's main bar, out of a fight. Turning on **Cast fill animation**, **Interrupt flash** or **Aim highlight** plays that effect once there. A slider you drag plays it once. Before, you saw these only on a real cooldown, cast, interrupt or ground spell.
- Fixed: **Aim highlight** no longer stops at the next Auto Shot or wand shot. A hunter aiming Volley or Flare, or a mage wanding while aiming Blizzard, now sees the button pulse until the spell is placed, cast, fails or is cancelled, as the game's buttons do.
- Fixed: pointing at Flat UI's page arrows now brings a faded main bar back to full at once, on the **Fading** page's **Main action bar** row, and the whole block with **Fade the block** on the **Bar block** page. Before, the arrows sit left of the bar, so it stayed faded, and at **Faded opacity** 0% you could not see the arrow you were about to click.
- Fixed: on Flat UI's buttons, the game glow of **Proc glow** no longer breathes over empty space past a bar's **Number of icons**. A hidden slot that still holds Overpower or Execute now stays dark; the glow shows and hides with its button.
- **Page arrows on the main bar**: a click now plays the game's soft scroll sound, and pointing at an arrow says **Next page** or **Previous page**, the page you are on and its key, as "Key: s-WU", short as the hotkeys are. Only the left button pages now, and a profile switch that turns the row on or off shows or hides the arrows at once. Before, a right-click paged too and a profile switch waited for a reload.
- New **Shortest cooldown that flashes** (0 s), with **Flash when ready** on the **Colours and cooldowns** page: a cooldown shorter than this ends without a flash, so a warrior's 6 second Mortal Strike stops flashing all fight and the flash of a 3 minute cooldown stands out. 0 flashes every cooldown longer than the global cooldown, as before. A cooldown already running at login or after a page change still flashes.
- New **Count colour** (white) and **Count position** (**Bottom right**, **Bottom left**, **Top left**, **Top right** or **Bottom**), on the **Button text** page: the count on an action button (reagents for Vanish or Rebirth, arrows on Auto Shot, a stack of potions) takes its own colour and can move out from under a bottom-right hotkey or a long macro name, on Flat UI's buttons and the restyled game buttons. A new position chosen in a fight takes effect when it ends. Before, the count could only be resized and was always white at the bottom right.
- **Interrupt flash** now flashes in the cast bar's **Interrupted colour** from the **Colours** page, so the button and the cast bar say "interrupted" in one colour. Before, it was always a fixed bright red.
- Fixed: with **Global cooldown sweep** off, a spell whose own cooldown ends under the global cooldown keeps its sweep to the end, so it no longer looks ready before you can press it, and its **Flash when ready** comes as that sweep ends. Buttons that only wait for the global cooldown still show no sweep.
- **Mark action buttons**: a button that two trackers share now stays marked while either is low or missing. It is red while either is missing and amber only when both are just low. Before, buffing yourself wiped the red border of a Power Word: Fortitude button while 2 of 5 group members still lacked it, and the colour depended on list order.
- **Edit a tracker** now has a **Sound** row under **Sound when it turns low**, so one tracker can have a sound of its own (a Soulstone tracker can chime while a missing buff plays the raid warning). It is dimmed while it follows the Trackers page, and has the Play button and play-on-pick of every sound dropdown.
- **Absorb shields**: out of combat with no shield up, changing **Show**, **Total bar is full at**, **Bar width**, **Bar height**, **Spacing**, **Bars grow**, **Bar texture**, **Time strip**, **Amount**, **Time left**, **Spell name**, **Colour by school** or **Shield colour** shows the sample bars in the new look for about 4 seconds, with no chat line. A slider drag keeps them up until 4 seconds after its last step. Picking a **When a shield breaks** sound with no shield up now shows the samples and flashes them red. Before, it flashed nothing.
- **Defensives on you** and **Your buffs**: out of combat with none up, changing **Icon size**, **Spacing**, **Caster's name** or **Icon border** (on Your buffs: **Time left**, **Icon size**, **Spacing**, **Icon border** or **Bar texture**) shows the three samples in the new look for about 4 seconds, with no chat line.
- **Trackers**: the countdown on a low icon is now amber, as the rim and count are and as the game's own icon colours its time in fights. It turns white again once the tracker is fine.
- New **Flash when one lands** (on/off, on by default) on **Defensives on you**: each defensive that lands, such as a Blessing of Protection or Pain Suppression, gives its icon a quick white flash. The game draws it, so it shows in fights too. The samples of `/flatui test icons` and test mode now flash as they appear and play **Sound when one lands**, instead of a Spell alert when each sample runs out.
- **Your buffs** and **Defensives on you**: the time left under or on an icon (Slice and Dice, Recklessness, Blessing of Protection) now turns amber in its last 5 seconds, also in fights, since the game colours it itself. Before, it stayed white to 0.
- **Close up the gaps**: when two trackers go away a moment apart (at a merchant, auto repair fixes **Durability** and the restock fixes **Ammunition** just after), the row now closes up again once the second fade is over. Before, the second tracker's empty place stayed until another tracker came or went.
- **Cooldown pulse**: out of combat, a pick in **Sound of one spell or item** or **Spot of one spell or item** now pulses that spell's or item's own icon and name at its spot, with its own sound (none plays nothing). Changing **Pulse spots** shows a test pulse at each spot in use. Before, both only printed a line.
- **Trackers**: picking a **Sound** on **Low and missing** now plays it once. Before, two copies played on top of each other.
- `/flatui tracker test`: every sample now shows its tooltip ('A sample of the test.') while **Tracker tooltips** allows it, also for trackers the test brings up from hidden, which is the usual case with **Only when low or missing**. When the test ends, each tracker takes the mouse again as before.
- **Group tools**: the button of the marker your target wears now lights up in that marker's colour, its colour behind it and on its border, and follows target and marker changes, in a fight too. Its tooltip adds 'On your target now: a left-click takes it off.' Before, all eight markers looked the same whatever your target wore. In test mode the Skull is lit as a sample.
- **Group tools**: the role poll's tooltip now lists who has which role ('Tank: Mara', 'Healer: Eli', 'Damage: Ivo, Fay') and, in amber, who has none. In a raid it gives the counts ('Tanks 2 · Healers 8 · Damage 28') and then who has no role. It updates as roles change, and test mode shows sample roles. Before, it only said 'Asks everyone in the group to pick a role.'
- **Fade until you point at it** on **Group tools** and the **Ping bar** now works in test mode: the buttons fade to 30% a second after the mouse leaves and come back at once under it. Before, they stayed full while test mode was on, the only place a player in no group sees them. A real ready check, countdown or waiting ping still keeps them full.
- **Ping bar**: the keys on the buttons ('M4', 's-F') keep a third of **Button size** after **Replace the game's fonts** sets its fonts. Before, they could come back at the font's own height after login and cover the ping icon. The **Threat list** and **Healer mana** titles and bar text keep their size after a **Fonts** change too.
- New **Bars stack** (Down or Up, Down by default) on the **Threat list** and **Healer mana** pages. With Up, the bottom of the window stays on the bottom of its box in `/flatui move`, so more bars push the title up. A list placed above your action bars or player frame no longer runs off the bottom of the screen in a raid.
- **Healer mana**: a drinking healer now shows the seconds left on the drink, '18s 47%', counting down until he stands, so you know when to pull. Where the game gives no time it still reads 'Drinking 47%'. The test mode Druid reads '18s 47%'.
- New **Sound** on the **Party cooldowns** page (None by default, the eight sounds of **Enemy cooldowns**): plays as a party icon's cooldown ends, at most once a second, and test mode's sample sweep plays it once. Before, the Ready again section had only **Flash when ready**.
- **Party cooldowns** test mode: the sample Ivo's row and label now fade to 35%, with Dead in his tooltips, so the fade of a dead or offline member can be seen before a wipe. A real member's row that holds samples now fades while he is dead or offline.
- **Threat list** and **Healer mana**: a bar that hid as the list shrank now takes its value at once when it shows again. Before, a Rogue who came back on row 4 at 10% slid down from the 63% he had when the bar hid.
- **Enemy cooldowns** page: the icon rows now come in the order of **Party cooldowns** (**Icon size**, **Icon border**, **Border colour by kind**, **Time left**, **Countdown number size**, **Icon tooltips**). **Border colour by kind** is greyed while **Icon border** is off, and both it and **Flash when ready** now have tooltips. **Group tools** now says **Button size**, as the **Ping bar** does.
- Bags: while you search, every section's title says how many of its items match, as "Trade goods  3 of 24" in the Highlight colour, open or folded ("3" alone where the longer text would not fit), and a section, or a bag's label in **Bag by bag**, with no match fades; the titles go back as they were when the search is cleared.
- **Currencies in the footer**: Shift-click a currency to link it in chat, also in a fight, or, with no chat box open, to take it off the footer as on the game's backpack; a click in a fight now opens the Currency tab once the fight ends, with one line in chat, and the tooltip names both clicks. Another character's currency only links.
- **Bag slots** and the bank window's bag row: a bag you clicked to show alone lets that go when you drag it out or swap it, so a new bag in that slot no longer comes back lit with every other item dimmed.
- Bags: after a fold, the bag window keeps its height only while your mouse is on it, so the empty band goes once the mouse leaves; the bank window simply shrinks as you fold.
- Bags: each change of your gold floats up from the footer as a short "+38s 2c" in green or "-1g 4s" in red, and a whole Sell junk pass adds up to one growing sum.
- Bags: the free slots in the footer pulse twice in red when your last general slot fills, or when the bag window opens full after it last had room.
- Bags: Up and Down in the search box bring back your recent searches, and Down goes forward again to what you had typed; a single stray letter is no longer kept as a recent search.
- **Bag slots**: the keyring's tooltip lists the keys it holds, each with its icon and name in its quality colour, by name (at most 12, then "and N more"), or says "No keys yet."
- **Flat UI bags: clean up** key: it works whenever the Windows page or the Bags page is on, and a press during a clean up says "Flat UI: your bags are still being cleaned up." instead of starting the sort again; the tooltips of the sort button, the bank button and the search box name the key you bound, or say where to bind one.
- New **Consumables** (One section / Food, potions and the rest) on the Bags page: By kind shows **Food and drink**, **Potions and elixirs** (flasks too) and **Consumables** for the rest, each folded, moved and dropped on like any other section.
- New **Gains over the bar** (on): each kill or hand-in now shows a small outlined "+1,240" that rises about 10 px from the end of the experience fill and fades over about 1.2 s, and a reputation gain shows "+75" over the reputation bar in its standing's colour. It shows in a fight too, and a quick second gain starts it again with the new amount. Logging in, a bar that shows again, a new level or standing and test mode's samples show none.
- Experience bar: Ctrl-click on the experience or reputation bar now counts the session from now, as `/flatui xp reset` does, so pace, time to level and reputation pace start fresh after an AFK break. Chat says "Flat UI: the experience and reputation count starts again now.", and the tooltip ends with "Ctrl-click: count this session from now".
- `/flatui move` in a fight (also the options window's **Move frames** row and test mode's **Move** button) now opens the movers as the fight ends, and chat says "Flat UI: /flatui move opens when the fight ends.". Before, it said "Movers open out of combat." and nothing opened. A second one in the same fight takes it back. Every chat line of the movers now starts with "Flat UI:".
- `/flatui move` toolbar: pointing at the grid's **Off**, **Dim** and **Bright**, **Snap**, **Samples**, **Frame list**, **Undo** and **Done** now says what each does. **Undo** says how many steps it holds ("Puts back the last change, 3 to undo."), and Ctrl-Z undoes the last move, size or reset while the movers are open.
- New **Durability shows** (Always, At 50% or less, At 20% or less; Always by default) on the **Minimap** page: the durability line under the map can now wait until an item wears to the mark, coming in yellow or red and going after a repair. The lines and addon button row below close up while it is hidden. The setup picture follows.
- New **While casting** (off) under **Stay visible** on the **Fading** page: faded frames are at full while you cast or channel, and for **Wait before fading** after, so a buff, meal, hearthstone or instant spell outside a fight brings the bars back. Under the mouse any faded frame is now back to full in a tenth of a second, and the fade down still takes **Fade time**. Before, both ways took **Fade time** (0.3 s by default).
- New **Sound before a roll ends** (None by default; Spell alert, Chimes, Bell, Warhorn, Raid warning, Alarm, Whisper warning) under **Seconds left on rolls**: plays once as a roll you have not rolled on reaches 5 seconds left, in a fight too. Several rolls ending together give one sound, and picking one plays it.
- New **While stunned, silenced or feared** (on/off, off by default) under **Hide error messages**: hides "Can't do that while stunned", "Can't attack while fleeing" and the like, also pacified, confused or charmed, with their sound. /flatui status counts it as one more kind.
- **Warn as the log fills**: a log that loads late after login, a reload or a loading screen, or that grows by more than two quests in one read, no longer says "Flat UI: your quest log is full (20/20)." or pulses. Only quests you accept fill it.
- Test mode: while no timer of the game's shows, a **Breath** bar now drains over 30 seconds and loops at the **Timer bars** spot. "Breath" is centred under it and its time ("0:30") counts down at its right, red under 10, as **Breath and fatigue timers** and **Seconds on the timers** draw the real one. With **Breath and fatigue timers** off it uses the game's look.
- Trinket queue: the square beside each trinket slot now updates its tooltip as soon as you click it, and counts the cooldown and 'Next: X in 0:45' down while you point at it. Before, it stayed as it was until you moved the mouse away and back. **Shift-click** a square for **Queue now** on that slot, with one line in chat ('Flat UI: the top trinket queue looks again now.'), so a red square no longer needs the window. A refused trinket now reads 'The game refused it: Queue now tries it again'.
- **Trinket queue window** now fades in over a tenth of a second with the character sheet's open sound, and plays the close sound as it shuts by its cross, Escape, `/flatui queue` or a slot's square, as the Gear comparison window does. It stays silent while `/flatui move` hides it and when the module goes off.
- Trinket queue window: an empty list now shows **Copy the Top trinket list (5)** (or Bottom) under 'No trinkets yet'. One click fills it in the same order, with each Delay and Stop. `/flatui queue copy bottom` (or `copy top`) does the same, and `/flatui queue clear top` (or `bottom`) empties a list with one line in chat.
- Trinket queue window: a cooldown at or under **Ready with this much left** now shows in the Ready colour, since the queue already counts that trinket as ready, and the row's tooltip says 'Counts as ready: 30 s or less left'. Before, 0:25 looked the same as 1:40.
- Trinket queue window: with the chat box open, Shift-click a trinket's icon in the list, the worn trinket's icon or one under **In your bags** to put its link in the chat line. It changes nothing else. With no chat box open, Shift-click keeps its window meaning.
- **Sound on a swap** and **Sound when you are named** now play the picked sound once while the options window is open, as the other sound rows do. **Sound on a swap** also gets a tip.
- New **Other words to colour** list under **Mentions** on the **Chat text** page: `/flatui words add tank` colours another word in other players' lines the same way as your name, with **Sound when you are named**, such as 'tank', your alt's name or your guild's raid call word. It matches whole words only, in any case, never inside links or web addresses, up to 20 for all your characters. `/flatui words` lists them, and `/flatui words remove tank` or choosing one in the row stops colouring it.
- New **Guild colour and rank** (on), on the **Tooltips** page under **Class colour on the class line**: a player's guild line is now green for your own guild and a soft blue for another, with the member's rank after the guild's name. Before, it was plain white.
- **Hint while Shift is up**: bags, quivers and items that cannot be worn no longer end with 'Shift: compare with what you wear', since Shift compares them with nothing. Tabards, shirts and relics now get the plain hint without 'and your spells', since no gear lines come for them.
- **Duration colour** on the **Buffs and debuffs** page has a third choice, **White, red in the last 10 s**: the time on a buff, debuff or totem turns red for its last ten seconds and is white again on a fresh one. A time the game withholds in a fight stays white, and test mode's sample auras follow it.
- **Data panels**: the **Reputation** slot now switches faction together with Flat UI's reputation bar when **Show the reputation you gain** follows a new faction, half a second after the gain. Before, it kept showing the old faction until your next reputation gain. Test mode's sample now reads "Stormwind Revered 88%".
- **Data panels**: a right-click on a slot now opens a small menu under it, titled with the panel and the slot ("Top panel, third slot"). It lists None, every slot kind and any LibDataBroker feeds, the one shown now in gold with a tick, and a pick changes the slot at once (Home's **Recent changes** and Ctrl+Z can undo it). Its last line, **Data panels options**, opens the page as the right-click did before. The tooltip hint now reads "Right-click: choose what this slot shows."
- **Data panels**: new slot **Ammunition**. With a bow, crossbow or gun it shows your arrows or bullets as "Ammo 1,840", in gold at or under the Trackers page's **Ammunition minimum** and in red at none or with ammunition that does not fit. A thrown weapon shows its stack. Its tooltip names the ammunition, the count in your bags and the free slots of your quiver or ammo pouch. A character with no ranged weapon that takes ammunition gets no slot.
- **Data panels**: new slot **Soul shards**: a warlock sees "Shards 12", red at none, with the soul bag's free slots in its tooltip. Other classes get no slot.
- **Data panels**: a click on the **Coordinates** slot now opens the world map, as the coordinates under the minimap do. In a fight it opens as the fight ends, with "Flat UI: the world map opens when the fight ends." Shift-click still puts "Elwynn Forest 45.2, 61.8" in chat, and the hint reads "Click: open the world map. Shift-click: put this in chat."
- **Mount key**: **Press a key to set it** now also takes the middle mouse button, the side buttons and the mouse wheel (with Shift, Ctrl or Alt if held) over the box that asks, and chat names them as "Flat UI: Mount key is Mouse Button 4." A left click or Escape cancels. A key that moves you, such as W or Space, is no longer taken at once: the box says "W moves you forward. Press it again to use it anyway, or press another key."
- **Cursor ring**: new choice **When you shake the mouse**. No ring shows until you shake the pointer from side to side. Then it fades in swollen, eases back to its size and fades out about a second later, and a shake while it fades swells it again. **Cursor ring size** and **Cursor ring colour** apply to it; it shows no cast or global cooldown, and test mode shows it whole.
- New **Release at once in battlegrounds** (on/off, off by default) on the **Popups and invites** tab of **Errands**: half a second after you die in a battleground your spirit goes to the graveyard and chat says "Flat UI: Released your spirit (battleground)." It does nothing while a soulstone, Reincarnation or another way back is offered, and never in an arena, the open world or a dungeon. While the game holds the release, as when you fall, it tries again.
- **Sell grey items**: holding Shift as a merchant opens now says so in chat, as "Flat UI: Kept 7 grey items this visit (Shift was held as the merchant opened).", only when there was something to sell and **Say what was done** is on. Before, the greys stayed with no word. The repair line is unchanged.
- **Lava tick timer**: the bar now fades in over about 0.15 s as it appears and fades out over 0.4 s once you leave the lava, like the hit table, ring and loot bars. A tick during the fade-out brings it straight back. Death, the row going Off and the end of test mode still hide it at once.

## 0.5.37

- For WoW: Forever 1.60.1 build 70938. The interface number stays 16001.
- New **Custom sections** in **Bags**: make sections of your own with names you choose, up to 20. Click **+ New section** after the last section of the bag window, choose **New section** from a section title's right-click menu, or choose **Make a new section** in the new **Custom sections** row on the **Bags** page. Then type a name of up to 24 letters and press Enter (Escape cancels). The box says in red why a name will not do: empty, too long, with a | in it, or already one of Flat UI's sections or yours. Drop an item on **+ New section** to make a section for that item. Your sections come after Keep in place and are the same in both windows and for all your characters. They move with **Section order**, fold with a click like the others, and the search finds them by name. The bag window always shows them, empty ones too, and the bank window shows one only while it holds bank items.
- **Custom sections**: right-click a section of yours for **Rename** and **Delete this section**, or right-click **+ New section** for all of yours with how many items each holds; the **Custom sections** row does the same with the page on or off. Deleting a section puts its items back in their own sections. The name box never opens in a fight and closes as one starts. `/flatui status` says how many sections you made, names the first five, and says how many items are placed in them.
- New **Drop slot in each section** (on) on the **Bags** page: in Sections, each open section of your bag window ends in an empty slot with a +. Drop an item on it, or click it with an item on your cursor, to keep that item in that section, as a drop on its title does: on Junk it goes on your sell list, on Keep in place its slot is pinned. While you hold an item a + slot takes, it lights in your **Highlight** colour. Letting go of an item anywhere among a section's slots does the same, in both windows and with the row off too, while a drop on an item still swaps the two.
- **Bags**: an item from your bank or one you wear, dropped on a + slot or among a section's slots in your bag window, goes into a free slot of your bags first, out of combat. Once it arrives it shows in that section instead of waiting under Added. It takes the first free slot of your backpack or normal bags that is not pinned, else a special bag it fits, and only last the slot pinned for that item, which is then unpinned so the item shows where you dropped it. A merchant's item dropped on a + slot is bought into a free slot the same way, except one the game asks about first (bought with items, or 150 gold or more). Nothing moves and nothing is bought when the section would not take the item, such as a quest item or one that cannot be sold on Junk, or once 500 items are placed by hand, and chat says why. An item still in the trade window, a bag from the bag bar, or an item your bags have no room for is refused with a line.
- **Bags**: **Free slots** now says how many slots of your backpack and normal bags are free out of all of them, as in 12 of 80: the count is gold at 4 or fewer and the whole title red when none is left. It stays as a title while your bags are full, and the + slots dim then but still take an item that is already in your bags. Its tooltip says what it counts.
- **Bags**: a section that took a drop, or that you just made or renamed, flashes in your **Highlight** colour and says what happened on a small tag beside its title, such as "Kept: Linen Cloth", "Moved in: Runecloth", "Bought: Refreshing Spring Water", "On your sell list" or "Pinned". The tag is as wide as its words, so a narrow section never cuts them.

## 0.5.36

- For WoW: Forever 1.60.1 build 70938. The interface number stays 16001.
- **Bags**: the bag window's footer now shows the currencies you tick **Show on Backpack** on the Currency tab of the Character window, next to your gold, in the game's order, each as its count and icon. Before, no currency showed anywhere while Flat UI bags were on, since the game's backpack that shows them stays hidden. Point at one for the game's tooltip of that currency, and click it to open the Currency tab, out of a fight. Another character's bags show its currencies as Flat UI last saw them. When the window is too narrow, the last ones lose their counts first, and the gold and free slots are never covered. New row **Currencies in the footer** (on) in the **Window** section of the **Bags** page.
- New **Trinket queue** (on), a page of its own under **Character and gear**: each trinket slot has its own list of your trinkets, best first. Tick **Top trinket slot** or **Bottom trinket slot** (both off until you do) and, whenever the trinket you wear is on cooldown, Flat UI puts on the highest one of that list you carry that is ready, and goes back to a higher one as soon as it is ready again. A trinket with 30 seconds or less of its cooldown left counts as ready (**Ready with this much left**), since putting on a trinket with a use starts a 30 second cooldown on it anyway. The game changes no trinket in a fight, so a swap waits for its end, and also while you cast, are dead, hold an item on the cursor or your bags are busy. The idea follows the trinket queue of TrinketMenu by Gello; none of its code is used.
- **Trinket queue window**: open it with `/flatui queue`, the row on the page or the small square right of each trinket slot on the Character window. Switch between **Top trinket** and **Bottom trinket**, click a trinket under **In your bags** to add it (Shift-click puts it first) or drop one on the window, and put them in order with the arrows. Give a trinket a **Delay** so its buff runs out before it comes off, tick **Stop** to keep it on while you wear it, and click its icon to wear it now: a copy already bound to you goes on first, so a spare that binds when worn stays unbound. **Pause all** stops every swap until you press it again, and **Queue now** lets the queue act at once. The window has a place of its own in `/flatui move` and a **Queue** button on the test mode bar.
- Trinket queue: a trinket you put on yourself, or with an equipment set, stays until you use it or press **Queue now**. A slot you empty yourself stays empty, so you can take a trinket off to bank, sell or trade it and the queue does not put it straight back. A trinket that would bind when worn is left for you to put on once. A trinket whose cooldown Flat UI cannot read stays on, and one the game will not let you wear is left out until you press **Queue now**, not tried again at each loot or fight.
- Trinket queue: the lists, each slot's **Auto queue** and **Pause all** are kept for each character, whatever profile is in use. A profile that switches by itself in a dungeon or a raid never turns the queue off, and each alt keeps its own.
- Trinket queue: **Buttons by the trinket slots** (on) puts a square beside each trinket slot. Its colour says whether that slot's queue is on, waits for a fight, a cast or a delay, is held or paused, or is off; point at it for why. Right-click it to turn that slot's queue on or off, Shift-right-click it to pause every swap, and drop a trinket on it to add it to that slot's list. **Say each swap in chat** (on) names the trinket put on, the one it replaced and why, **Sound on a swap** can play a sound, and `/flatui status` says what each slot waits for.
- Every sound dropdown, such as **Jump sound**, **Warning sound**, **Sound for a debuff you can dispel** or a page's **Sound**, now plays the sound once as you pick it, out of a fight. A new **Play** button right of it plays the sound chosen now and is dimmed at None. This works on the options pages, in setup's **More settings** and in the tracker editor.
- Where the game blocks reloads for add-ons, every **Reload** button (the options window's bottom strip, Home's **Needs a reload**, the reload notice, the welcome screen) is now dimmed in a fight. A press says "Flat UI: Reload works here again after the fight. Type /reload to reload now." instead of doing nothing, and the button comes back after the fight. Flat UI's other chat lines here now start with "Flat UI:" too.
- Escape with the colour picker open now cancels the colour and leaves the options window open, and a second Escape closes the window. Enter applies the picked colour. In setup, Escape now closes an open dropdown, question or the colour picker before the setup window.
- **Delete profile** now asks 'Delete "Raid" for good?' and says how many other characters use the profile and that they start as new characters at their next login, with **Delete** and **Keep** buttons. **Reset profile** now says that every setting and every frame position goes back, with **Reset** and **Keep**. Enter no longer answers these questions, nor **Copy** or the import's **Replace**, so an Enter meant for chat cannot delete or overwrite a profile.
- **Switch automatically**: picking a profile for the kind of place you are in now asks, for example, "You are in a raid now. Switch to Raid now?", with **Switch now** and **Later**. Before, nothing happened until you left and came back.
- Import: with **Frame positions** off, the Screen line ('Check the positions after the import') and **Fit positions to this screen** no longer show, since no position is imported. The count of a part left out is now dimmed.
- Ctrl+Z on any options page now puts back the newest change, and the strip says what, as "Undid Border colour: back to #000000." or "Nothing to undo.". Each press goes one change further back in Home's **Recent changes**.
- Search now finds the window's own actions under **Actions**: **Test mode** (**Turn on** or **Turn off**), **Move frames**, **Welcome screen** and **Ready-made looks**, with the typed words lit. Enter runs the picked one.
- While test mode is on and nothing needs a reload, a dimmed sample reload notice ("Sample: 2 changes need a reload: Square icons, Side tabs.", with greyed Reload, Undo and Later) now shows at the notice's spot and steps clear of the same bars. A real change replaces it, and it goes when test mode ends.
- New **Also on target, focus and boss frames** (on/off, off by default), under **Low health border** on **Unit frames** > **All frames: bars and pictures**: your target's, focus's and boss frames' health bar border turns the **Low health border colour** below **Low health below** (20% at first), for Execute and Hammer of Wrath, beats with **Pulse the low health border** and hides on a dead unit. In setup's **Bars, heals and borders** step the Defias Pillager drops to 15% with the border while both rows are on.
- **Pulse the low health border**: every low border on screen now dims and brightens together in one calm rhythm, and a frame the game hides plays nothing. Before, each border beat from the moment its frame first painted, so three low raid members throbbed out of step. Setup's **Bars, heals and borders** drawing and test mode's Ivo, Bex and the two raid members at 14% now beat too while the row is on. Before, the row changed nothing there.
- New **Border while it is not your target** (on/off, on by default) and **Not your target colour** (amber), under **Pet target frame** on the **Pet frame** tab: the pet target frame's border turns amber while your pet attacks something other than your target, you having none included, and goes back once you are both on the same unit, also in a fight. Test mode's Training Dummy shows it.
- **Pet target frame**: its box in `/flatui move` and test mode's **Move** is now as wide as the frame at any **Pet target width** and follows a width change at once. With no spot saved, a wider frame grows to the right from the same left edge. Before, the box stayed 120 px wide, so at 240 it covered half the frame and at 60 it stuck out past its end.
- **Pet target frame**: its bar and words fade in over about 0.15 s as it appears. Before, they popped on. **Druid mana in forms**: the mana bar fades in as you shift into Cat or Bear Form and fades out over 0.2 s as you shift back.
