- **Druid mana in forms**: with **Frame style** **Separate parts** and the Player frame's **Power bar** off, the mana bar now hangs right under the health bar, the usual two-border gap below it, as it already did in **One frame**. Before, it hung about 12 px lower, under the invisible power bar.
- **Squares for your heals over time**: a priest now also sees a white square for their Power Word: Shield and a yellow one for their Prayer of Mending, which moves with the bounce, and a shaman an orange square for their Earth Shield, each in its own place in the row. Test mode's squares match.
- **Sound for a debuff you can dispel**: picking a sound in the options window plays it once, out of a fight, as **No target sound** and the combat sounds already do. **None** plays nothing.
- Setup draws the dispel border and the heal over time squares: in the **Auras, flags and icons** step Mara, who carries a Magic debuff, shows the blue border inside her health bar and the Magic icon at its right end with **Border colour of a debuff you can dispel** and **Debuff type icon on party frames**. **Squares for your heals over time**, **Square corner** and **Square size** moved to the **Party frames** step, where Bex shows two squares in the corner and size you pick. Before, these rows were plain sentences and the drawing never changed.
- **Nameplates**: quest marks now show in dungeons, and while Questie's own nameplate icons are off, even with **Leave quest marks to Questie** on. Before, with Questie loaded, quest enemies in Deadmines or Wailing Caverns had no mark at all, since Questie draws none inside an instance on this game and Flat UI stood down anyway. Now they get Flat UI's ! and kill count while Questie draws none, the marks switch as you zone in and out, and setup and test mode show no sample mark while the marks are left to Questie.
- **Nameplates**: the threat value and the quest mark no longer sit on the game's row of enemy buffs left of the bar, and the threat value, the name of the enemy's target and a **Right** quest mark no longer sit on a polymorphed, sapped or stunned enemy's crowd control icon. Now the left reads ! 85% [buffs] marker bar and the right reads bar level [CC] 85% [Name] !, each moving out as icons come and go, in a fight too. With **Show auras** at **Only on the target**, the other plates keep no room for auras you cannot see.
- **Nameplates**: the **Overpower** icon, and a quest mark at **Quest mark position** **Above**, now sit just over the enemy's debuffs (your Rend, Sunder Armor or Hamstring) and rise as the row wraps to a second line. Before, the 34-unit icon covered some of them for each 5-second window. With no debuffs they stay where they were. Test mode's sample now shows its three auras with the icon above them.
- **Nameplates**: the **Overpower** icon pops in as an enemy dodges you, with a quick fade in and a slight shrink from 130%, so you see which enemy it was, and fades away over a quarter of a second when its 5 seconds run out. Casting Overpower, the enemy's death or its plate leaving still clear it at once.
- **Kill count beside the quest mark** picks the right objective when one enemy's name starts another's. Before, on a quest that kills both Plaguehound Runts and Plaguehounds, a Plaguehound's plate could show the Runts' count, such as 14/20 instead of 3/10. Now each enemy shows its own count.
- **Kill count beside the quest mark** pops as a kill counts: the count on every other plate of that enemy grows and settles back over a third of a second, white until it settles, then gold, or grey on a tapped enemy. Test mode's sample counts 3/8 to 7/8 with its threat rounds and pops each time.
- New **Flash the ring when threat goes wrong** (on), on the **Threat** tab of **Nameplates** under **Glow and opacity**: an enemy's threat ring flashes twice as it turns to you, or away from you while you tank, also in a fight, so it stands out among eight plates. Test mode's sample flashes each time it reaches You do not want.
- **Fade enemies held for you** eases instead of snapping: an enemy that goes from held for you (75%) to changing hands (100%) and back now glides to its new opacity over about 0.2 seconds, so plates no longer blink as threat moves during a pull. A plate that leaves, the game's own opacity change and a change of the rows still set it at once.
- Test mode's sample nameplate now shows the threat rows it left out: its name and health text take each state's colour with **Threat colour on the name** and **Health text in the name's colour**, its border with **Threat colour on the health bar border**, and its background with **Health bar background in the threat colour**. With **Name of the enemy's target** on, a [Gorvak] in the warrior colour sits right of the bar, your own name on You do not want unless **Not when it attacks you** hides it, and a **Right** quest mark moves past it.
- Test mode: with **Moving** off on the test mode bar, the dark swipe on a warrior's sample **Overpower** icon now holds still with the cast fill and threat states, and runs on from where it stopped when **Moving** is back on. Before, it kept draining and restarting every 5 seconds.
- **Spell rank on the player bar** now also shows in a fight: a cast started in combat reads "Flash Heal (Rank 4)" as one out of combat does, also where the game protects the cast bar then (Edit Mode's **Lock to player frame**). Before, every cast started in a fight showed the plain name, and `/flatui status` said a rank cast in a fight is not shown.
- New **Pushback on your bar** (on), on the **Text and icon** tab of **Cast bars**: a small red "+0.5" just past the right end of your cast bar adds up how far hits have set the cast back, and a channel cut short shows "-0.8". It clears as the cast ends. Setup's cast bar picture shows "+0.4" on the Player bar, and test mode shows it over the second half of your sample cast and channel.
- **Cast time on your bars** and **Cast time on target and focus bars** show the seconds from the first cast of a fight, also on a bar the game protects then (Edit Mode's **Lock to player frame**). Before, a first cast of the session in a fight left the seconds empty for the whole fight. `/flatui status` counts casts that still had none, as after a reload in a fight.
- New **Fight length on -Combat** (on), under **Warnings** on the **Combat text** page: -Combat says how long the fight lasted, as "-Combat  1:24" ("-Combat  0:07" for a short pull). Test mode shows "-Combat  0:42", and `/flatui status` names the last fight's length.
- New **Name the key to target an enemy** (on), under **Warnings** on the **Combat text** page: **No target** names the key bound to Target Nearest Enemy, as "No target  (Tab)". The key is read each time the warning shows, so a new binding counts. Nothing is added with no key bound, or while you heal.
- New **Low health and mana warning** (on) with **Low health colour**, under **Warnings** on the **Combat text** page: **Low health** shows under No target, breathing the same way, while your health is under 20%, and **Low mana** under it while your mana is, also in a fight. These are the game's own Low Health and Low Mana, which went away when **Combat text for you** turned off its Scrolling Combat Text for Self. Test mode shows Low health under the sample No target.
- **Combat text** preview in setup and the options window: the Outgoing column now stays whole inside the picture at any **Outgoing style** and size. Before, the first number touched the right edge at the defaults, and with Horizontal or a size of 250% to 400% numbers were cut in half or vanished past the edge.
- `/flatui combat test` and test mode send the Outgoing area a Fire spell crit with the Fireball icon, and give icons only as real hits get them: the swing's with **Spell icons** on **All**, the spell's on **All** or **Spells only**, both only while **Whose hits** is **Yours**. Before, the swing kept its icon with Spells only and no spell icon ever showed.
- Interrupt colours can be seen without a fight: setup's cast bar picture shows the Target bar in **Interrupt ready colour** and the Focus bar in **Interrupt on cooldown colour**, and test mode shows the target's sample cast ready and the focus's in **Interrupt out of range colour**, while **Interrupt readiness on enemy casts** and **Flat fill** are on. Before, every sample bar was green.
- Test mode: for a warrior, druid or hunter the main hand swing timer turns **Queued ability colour** over the second half of each swing and takes its own colour back as the swing lands, as in a fight. Before, only setup's picture showed it.
- Flat UI's action buttons are no longer repainted in full on every rage, energy or mana change in a fight. Before, each power tick repainted all 96 buttons, which could cost frame rate on busy pulls. They now repaint slot by slot as their usability, range or cooldown changes, and look the same.
- New **Page arrows on the main bar** (on), under **Buttons** on the **Action bars** page: with Flat UI's buttons the page number and its up and down arrows are back left of the main bar, where the game has them. The number follows the page as you change it with Shift and the mouse wheel or a key, and the arrows page the bar, in a fight too. Before, they were hidden with the game's bar, so a page changed by mistake gave no sign and had no arrow to click back. Edit Mode's **Hide bar scrolling** hides them too.
- New **Aim highlight** (on), on the **Glows and animations** page: while a spell you place on the ground, such as Blizzard, Rain of Fire, Flamestrike, Hurricane or Volley, waits for its spot, its button on Flat UI's bars pulses softly in the cast bar's **Cast colour** until you place or cancel it. Before, the button showed nothing.
- New **Global cooldown sweep** (on), on the **Colours and cooldowns** page: with it off, a button of Flat UI's that only waits for the global cooldown shows no sweep, so only real cooldowns sweep and the bars stay calm between presses.
- New **Sweep colour** (with **Own sweep opacity**), **Edge line** (off) with its **Edge colour**, and **Flash colour** (with **Flash when ready**), on the **Colours and cooldowns** page: the sweep on action buttons can take any colour, a bright line can run along its edge as on the cooldown icons, and the ready flash can be gold or any colour in place of white.
- **Cooldown sweep**, **Own sweep opacity**, **Sweep colour**, **Cooldown number size**, **Cooldown number colour** and **Tenths below** now apply to the restyled pet and stance bars too. Before, a pet's Spell Lock or Dash and stance cooldowns kept the game's numbers and dark sweep beside restyled buttons.
- Flat UI's buttons now follow the game's **Show numbers on cooldowns**, at once as you turn it on or off. Before, the numbers always showed on Flat UI's bars, even with the game's option off.
- New **Full opacity as you cast** (off), on the **Bar block** page: each spell or item you use brings a faded block back to full while you cast and for **Wait before fading** after, so a buff, a meal or an opening cast outside a fight shows its cooldown.
- **Keybind on icons** of the cooldown icons now shows the key that casts the spell now. Before, a spell on main-bar page 1 and on a stance bar showed the page 1 key in that stance, and a stance or page change kept the old key until the bindings changed.
- The cast fill, the interrupt flash, the new aim highlight and **Flash when ready** now light only buttons you can see. Before, after you lowered a bar's number of icons in Edit Mode, they could flash over empty space or over another bar moved there.
- **Trackers**: a low tracker is now amber and a missing one stays red. Amber covers the rim, the count, and the bar's fill and text, the game's own icon in fights (count, time left and low rim) and the **Mark action buttons** border. Before, low and missing were the same red, so "renew soon" looked like "gone".
- **Trackers**: **Hide while mounted** is now **Hide while mounted or flying**, and also hides the trackers set to **Only when low or missing** on a flight path until you land. Before, every missing buff, low ammunition and durability tracker showed red for the whole flight.
- New **Close up the gaps** (off), on **Trackers** > **Look and position**: a hidden tracker takes no room, so the ones that show sit together, centred or from the start of the row or column as **Row direction** says. A tracker that fades out keeps its place until it is gone. In a fight nothing moves, and a tracker that shows there takes a place after the others.
- Fixed: with **Tracker tooltips** at **On**, a tracker you cannot see no longer takes the mouse or shows a tooltip in a fight. This covers a proc that is down and a tracker the game's own icon hides. Before, an empty patch of screen showed 'The game hides it in a fight' and blocked the mouseover of a unit under it.
- **Trackers**: a new **Bar texture** now reaches the bars the game draws in fights as soon as the game shows your buffs again. Before, those kept the old texture until you logged in again.
- **Tracker tooltips**: a dim line now says what counts as low, such as "Low at 2 charges or fewer or under 5:00 left (earlier in a raid)", "Low under 5 in your bags" or "Low at 200 or fewer". The members who lack a group buff are named in their class colours, and a weapon with no enchant reads "No enchant on it".
- **Trackers**: picking a **Sound** plays it once out of combat, so you hear the choice before a buff drops.
- New **Bar tooltips** (On, Out of combat only, Off; On by default), on **Absorb shields**. Pointing at a shield's bar shows the game's tooltip of its buff with what it has left, and pointing at the total bar lists each shield on you with its amount and time left. In a fight the game's own rows have the game's tooltip. A click still goes through to the world.
- **Absorb shields**: picking a sound in **When a shield breaks** plays it once out of combat, and the bars shown (real or test samples) flash red with it, so you see the whole break warning before a shield breaks.
- **Cooldown pulse**: out of combat, changing **Icon size**, **How long it shows**, **Opacity**, **Sound**, **Name under the icon**, **Grow as it fades** or the size of spot 2 or 3 now shows a test pulse at once at each spot in use, in the new look. A **Sound** picked plays with it. Before, nothing showed unless test mode was on or you typed /flatui pulse test.
- New **Sound when one lands** (None by default; Raid warning, Alarm, Whisper warning), on **Defensives on you**. It plays once as a listed defensive from someone else, such as Blessing of Protection or Pain Suppression, comes on you, also in a fight, because the game plays it itself. Spells you know yourself are left out, so a priest's own Power Word: Shield stays silent.
- **Threat list**: as a damage dealer or healer, once you hold the enemy's aggro your bar now keeps a steady red wash. Before, the red pulse of **Warn near aggro** stopped the moment you pulled, so your bar looked safe at the worst time. Warning sound now also plays then if it has not for that enemy, so a sudden pull from under **Warn at** (a big crit or a heal) is heard too. In test mode your sample bar takes the aggro every 10 seconds.
- **Threat list**: the title shows the enemy's raid marker, such as a Skull or a Cross, before its name and follows marker changes in a fight, so you can tell which of three Defias Pillagers the list is about. The sample shows a Skull.
- **Threat list** and **Healer mana**: bars glide to each new value instead of jumping once a second or stepping down every half second. A bar that turns to another member, a new enemy or a list that just appeared takes its value at once, so a bar never slides between two people.
- **Threat list** test mode for tanks: while your role reads as tank, your sample bar holds the aggro at 100%, and every 10 seconds a sample Mage takes it for one step. Your bar then pulses red for 2 seconds and Warning sound plays once, as **Warn when you lose aggro** does in a fight. Before, a tank's test mode never showed a warning.
- **Healer mana**: out of combat the title reads the average mana of the living healers shown, such as "Healer mana 64%", in red while it is under **Low mana at**. In a fight, where the game hides the numbers, it reads Healer mana. The sample reads 48%.
- **Party cooldowns** and **Enemy cooldowns**: the white flash of **Flash when ready** now fades out smoothly and ends cleanly. Before, it could end with a one-frame white blink, and a second flash within half a second was cut short by the first one's timer.
- **Party cooldowns** in a raid group (and the test mode stack): each row's name is now in its class colour, yours too, right-aligned on one line. Before, the names were the game's gold, and a long name wrapped onto the next row's label.
- **Party cooldowns**: the row of a dead, ghost or offline member fades to 35% with its name and comes back when they are up again, also in a fight, so a wipe no longer shows bright Ready icons nobody can use. The icon tooltip adds Dead or Offline.
- New **Fade until you point at it** (off), on the **Group tools** page and on the **Ping bar** page: the buttons stay faint (30%) until the mouse comes over any of them, come back to full at once and fade again a second after the mouse leaves. They still take clicks and keys, in a fight too. Group tools stays full during a ready check or a countdown, and the ping bar while a ping waits.
- **Ping bar**: a ping that waits now gives up after 10 seconds out of combat. The button's light and the line by the bar go, and the cursor comes back. One left waiting through a fight is cancelled as the fight ends. Before, one stray click on a ping button made the next left click anywhere, on an action button, a bag or an NPC, send a ping instead. The tooltip says so.
- **Bags**, Sections: clicking a section's title to fold it or open it again now keeps the bag window's height while it stays open. The title stays under your mouse, and its tooltip changes at once to "Folded: click to open it." with what the section holds. The same goes for Shift-click and the title menu's **Fold**, **Unfold**, **Fold every section** and **Unfold every section**. Before, the window shrank or grew at its top, so the title you clicked jumped away from the mouse by the height of the rows that folded.
- **Bag slots**: a bag's tooltip now opens beside the bag window, on the side of the screen with room (left of it while the window sits on the right), its bottom level with the window's. The bank window's row of bank bags does the same. Before, the tooltip covered the window's bottom rows, the very items the bag lights.
- **Bag slots**: the row now ends with your keyring while the game shows one, with the game's keyring icon and the number of keys in its corner. Drop a key on it, or click it with a key on your cursor, and the key goes into the first free keyring slot. A plain click shows only the keyring, as the other slots do, and its tooltip says how many keys it holds. Before, the row had no keyring, so with **Hidden frames** > **Bag bar** on, a looted key could not be put on the keyring in Sections.
- **Bag slots**: while a spare bag sits in your bags, each empty bag slot it fits stays lit. Its tooltip says "Click to put in Linen Bag (6 slots) from your bags.", and a click puts it in, largest first, out of a fight. The reagent bag slot offers only a reagent bag. Before, a click on an empty slot did nothing.
- **Bag slots**: a bag with 4 free slots or fewer now shows its count in gold, as the footer's free slots do, and in red once it is full. Before, the count was white until the bag was full.
- Bank window: the row of bank bags now works like **Bag slots**. Each bank bag shows its free slots in its corner (gold at 4 or fewer, red when full), and an empty bought slot shows the game's faded bag art. A plain click shows only that bag in the bank window, its edge in the **Highlight colour**, until you click it again. A drag picks the bag up, a click or drop with a bag on the cursor puts it in, and the tooltip says so. Before, every click lifted the bag onto the cursor.
- Bag search: the search box's right-click menu now ends with **Recent**, your last five searches, newest first, with the one typed now ticked. A click types it again, and **Clear recent** empties the list. They are kept for the account between sessions; quick searches are not added to it. Before, a search you typed yourself was gone once the bags closed.
- **Item marks**: in Flat UI's bag and bank windows, a recipe this character can learn now (the right profession and skill, not known yet, no red line) has a small green plus at its bottom left. The new search word #learnable, and the new quick search **Recipes you can learn**, light them all. The game's own bag windows are unchanged.
- Bag search: while you search, the box shows how many items match at its right, just left of the cross (for example 12), for the character that window shows. It turns red at 0, and the box's tooltip says "12 items match". The number agrees with the character picker's count.
- **Items placed by hand**: the list now starts with "Put every item back in its own section" while two or more items are placed. With none, it says how to place an item. Before, items went back one at a time and an empty list showed nothing.
- New key **Flat UI bags: clean up** under Flat UI in Key Bindings: it cleans up your bags with the game's clean-up sound, as the sort button and /flatui sort do, in a fight too, and the bag window's sort button looks busy as after a click.
- Loot rolls now count down the whole seconds left at the right end just above the timer ("39", "38" ...). Under 10 seconds the number and the timer bar turn red, and the number goes away as the roll ends or the Need animation plays. The setup picture and test mode's sample rolls show it too. New **Seconds left on rolls** (on/off), on by default on the **Quests and loot** page.
- The breath, fatigue and feign death bars now show the time left ("1:12") at the right under the bar, with the label still centred. While the bar drains the time turns red under 10 seconds; it stays white while your breath comes back. The setup picture's "Breath 1:12" now shows the time only with the row on. New **Seconds on the timers** (on/off), on by default on the **Messages and timers** page.
- Text around the minimap: a click on the durability line opens the Character window, and a click on the coordinates opens the world map; Shift-click still puts them in chat. In a fight both wait for its end with one chat line, as the time line does, and the tooltips end with the matching hints. The time tooltip now also gives "This session 1h 20m".
- Flat UI's experience bar, with **Animate gains** on: a new level now lights the whole bar once in soft gold, fading over about a second as the fill drops to its new value. A new standing with the same faction (Friendly to Honored) flashes the bar in the new standing's colour.
- **Opacity in combat** now eases the objective tracker (or Questie's) down over about a quarter of a second as a fight starts and back up over about 0.2 seconds as it ends, where it used to jump each way. Where the **Fading** page's **Objective tracker** rule takes the tracker back, it eases on from the fight's opacity.
- **Quest count** turns amber at one free slot. When the log becomes full during play the count gives a short red pulse, and chat says once "Flat UI: your quest log is full (20/20)."; the first count after login or a reload never warns. New **Warn as the log fills** (on/off), on by default on the **Quests and loot** page.
- Flat UI setup's **Minimap** step now draws the coordinates, durability, frame rate and latency, and time lines above or below the mock map, as their place rows say. They change with Decimals, Show, Latency, Colour by value, Local or realm time, Time format and **Text size**, and vanish when their row is off.
- Flat UI setup's **Fading** step has new **Bag windows** and **Experience bar** tiles that fade to **Faded opacity** when their row is ticked.
- Every number on Flat UI's experience bar and in setup is now grouped the same way: **As the game has it** shows "12,150 / 27,000", the reputation line "Stormwind 4,200 / 12,000", and the setup picture "3,700 / 10,000 (37%)" and "6,300 to go".
- The experience bar tooltip now reads "Rested: 8,000 (30% of the level)" in **Rested colour**. With **Bar text** at **Percent** or **Value and percent**, "+30%" follows in Rested colour while you are rested. New **Rested share in the text** (on/off), on by default on the **Experience and menu bars** page.
- New **Dark quest, gossip and book pages** (on), in **Window art** on the **Windows** page: the quest giver, gossip, book and letter pages and the quest log details are now dark with light text, like the other restyled windows. They are the game's own Quest Text Contrast 'Dark' pages, set for your account; turned off, the parchment and the contrast you had before come back. A page already open changes the next time it opens.
- New **Your name in chat** (on), under **Mentions** on the **Chat text** page: your name in other players' say, yell, emotes, party, raid, guild, officer and numbered channel lines (such as Trade) now shows in a bright colour, **Your name colour** (orange by default). Only the whole name counts, any case, never inside a link or web address, and not in whispers or your own lines. New **Sound when you are named** (None by default, or Spell alert, Chimes, Bell and the other usual sounds) plays once for each line that names you.
- **Next rank** no longer says 'you can learn it now' for ranks no trainer teaches: the ranks from the Ahn'Qiraj class books (Frostbolt 11, Fireball 12, Renew 10, Healing Touch 11, Heroic Strike 9, Shadow Bolt 10 and the rest) now read, dimmed, 'Rank 11 comes from a book, not a trainer'.
- **Who in your group targets it** now updates while you keep pointing at a unit: names come and go within 0.2 s as members change target, the line reads 'Targeted by: no one' once nobody has it, and it appears as soon as a first member takes a unit nobody had.
- New **Health bar colour** (Class colour for players, reaction for the rest), on the **Tooltips** page: the health bar under a unit's tooltip is now a player's class colour and the reaction colour of any other unit (a hostile mob red, a neutral one yellow), kept through every health change, also in a fight. **Green, as the game has it** gives the game's green back.
- **Average and rates** now ends with how many casts a full bar of mana gives, as '23 casts from full mana', so two ranks of Frostbolt, or Flash Heal and Heal, compare at a glance. Rage and energy spells get none.
- **Explain the stats** speaks with one voice: Hit Chance, Critical Strike, Expertise and the weapon damage glance lines now use the same 'Level 60 · Boss 63' heads, ' · ' between values and 'From before the fight' line as Defense and Dodge. The boss column follows the same rule (from level 60, or with **Against** set to a boss); a lower level character sees one 'Level 30' column, and hit and expertise still wanted give one figure.
- **Hit table under the player frame** now fades in over 0.15 s and out over 0.35 s as an enemy is targeted, lost or killed, or a fight begins or ends. Targeting again during the fade-out brings it back at once; test mode and turning the row off still hide it at once.
- The **Gear comparison** window now fades in over a tenth of a second with the character sheet's open sound and plays its close sound when it shuts (its cross, **Compare gear**, `/flatui gear` or Escape). The item finder fades in beside it.
- New **Hint while Shift is up** (on), under **Compare items only while holding Shift**: an item you could wear now ends its tooltip with a dim 'Shift: compare with what you wear' ('and your spells' while the gear lines wait for the comparison), which goes as you hold Shift. Items you wear get none.
- **Data panels**: a click on **Time**, **Quest log**, **Friends online**, **Guild online**, **Durability** or **Reputation** in a fight now opens its window as the fight ends. Chat says so once, as in "Flat UI: the quest log opens when the fight ends." (or the character window, the stopwatch, the calendar, your friends list, the guild window, your reputations). The tooltip's hint now reads "Click: open the quest log, after the fight." Before, the click did nothing, with no message. A press let go off the slot is no longer a click.
- **Data panels**: the **Durability** slot reads "Durability 92%  1 broken" in red while any item is at 0%. Otherwise it still counts the items at 50% or less. Its tooltip, also on the minimap's durability line, opens with a red "1 item broken: no stats until repaired" and lists the lowest item first, as the Character window does. Where the game's rules keep gear from wearing, the slot takes no room.
- **Data panels**: new **Reputation** choice for a slot. It shows "Stormwind Honored 35%" in the standing's colour, for the faction Flat UI's reputation bar shows (with **Show the reputation you gain**, the one you last gained with), else the one the game watches. With none, it takes no room. The tooltip has the standing, 4,200 / 12,000, what is left to the next standing and, for the bar's faction, the reputation pace and the time to the next standing. A click opens your reputations, after the fight in one. Shift-click puts "Stormwind: Honored 4,200 / 12,000 (35%)" in chat.
- **Mount key**: new **Key** row. Pick **Press a key to set it**, and a prompt in the middle of the screen asks for the key (Escape cancels). The next key, with Shift, Ctrl or Alt if you hold one, becomes the Mount key, and chat says "Flat UI: Mount key is Shift-Z.", adding "(it was Toggle Sheathe; that comes back when Mount key is off)" when the key had a binding before. **Clear Flat UI's key** forgets it. The row's line shows the key in use now. Before, the key could only be set under Flat UI in the game's Key Bindings, and only `/flatui status` said none was bound. A key bound there still works.
- **Train all**: the tooltip lists each skill with its rank and price, as "Frostbolt (Rank 3)    12s", up to 8 of them and then "and 3 more". The chat line names what you learn: "Flat UI: Learning 4 skills for 1g 20s: Frostbolt (Rank 3), Fireball (Rank 2) and 2 more." Before, both gave only a count.
- New **Say when a macro changes** (on), on the **Drink and health macros** page. Chat says when a macro moves to another drink, food, potion or healthstone, as "Flat UI: Flat UI Drink now drinks Morning Glory Dew." or "Flat UI: Flat UI Health now takes Major Healthstone, then Superior Healing Potion.", or when it runs out ("Flat UI: Flat UI Drink has nothing left to drink in your bags."). It says nothing at login, for a row you change on the page, or when a healthstone or mana gem on cooldown only changes the order.
- **Glow on the nearest object** fades in over a quarter of a second as it lands on a herb, vein or quest object. Before, it popped on at full strength.
- New **Glow sound** (None), under **Glow on the nearest object** on **World and cursor**, with the **Jump sound** choices (Spell alert, Chimes, Bell and the rest). It plays once as the glow lands on an object, out of a fight and at most once every 2 seconds, so a herb that comes into reach rings softly. Picking one plays it.
- **Lava tick timer**: picking a **Jump sound** now plays it once while the options window is open and you are out of a fight, as the combat sounds do. None stays silent. Before, you heard it only in lava or in test mode.
- New **Grow when you shake the mouse** (on), under **Cursor ring**: a quick side-to-side shake swells the ring to two and a half times its size and eases it back over half a second, so you find the pointer at once. It works with **Always** and **In combat only**.
- **Maximum camera distance**: ticking it now glides the camera out to the new farthest distance, so you see the extra room at once. Before, nothing changed on screen until you scrolled. Unticking leaves the camera to the game, and nothing moves at login or at a profile change.

## 0.5.35

- For WoW: Forever 1.60.1 build 70938. The interface number stays 16001.
- **Character** window: point at a stat on the stats list and a few plain lines, under **In plain words**, now follow the game's own with your numbers as they are now. **Strength**, **Agility** and **Intellect** say what one point gives you, as "1% crit takes 20 Agility" or "1 more melee damage a second takes 7 Strength". **Stamina** and **Health** say how much of your health comes from Stamina. Mana and energy say how long they take from empty to full. **Spirit** shows your mana back while spending none and while casting, and explains the five-second rule. **Movement Speed** says how many yards a second you run and swim. A warlock's pet's mana regen says what each point of your Intellect gives it.
- **Character** window: **Hit Chance**, **Critical Strike** and **Expertise** show your chance to miss, to crit and to be dodged against an enemy of your level and against a raid boss three levels above, plus the hit and expertise you still need for none. They count these as the **Hit table** page does (**Attacks**, **Hit cap against a boss**). Weapon damage says what a hit does on average, what attack power adds a second and how many of your swings glance. **Attack Power** says how much comes from Strength and Agility. **Haste** says how often you swing and how long a 3 sec cast takes. **Spell Piercing** says the damage it adds against a creature of your level. Spell damage and spell healing point to the spell tooltips for each spell's share. In a fight, where the game hides your stats, the lines show the numbers from before it and say so.
- **Explain the stats** (on), new in the **Character window** section of **Item slots**: point at a stat on the Character window and, under the game's own lines, Flat UI says in plain words and your own numbers what it does for you. **Defense** shows what happens to 100 melee swings from an enemy of your level and, from level 60 or with **Against** set to a boss on the **Gear comparison** page, from a boss three levels above: how many miss you, are dodged, parried or blocked, crit for 200% damage, crush for 150% or hit normally. It also shows how far you are from the two marks a tank aims for: 440 defense, at which a raid boss can no longer crit you, and 100% of its swings missed, dodged, parried or blocked, at which it lands no crushing blows. **Dodge**, **Parry** and **Block** say how many of 100 swings each stops against both enemies. **Block** also says how much one block stops and how much of that comes from your shield and your Strength. **Armor** says what share of physical damage it takes off against both and what 100 more would add, and each resistance says what share of that school's damage it resists on average and what 10 more would add. Before, the game's tooltips gave chances against an enemy of your level only and said nothing about bosses, crushing blows or where block value comes from. In a fight the lines keep the numbers from before it and say so.
- Fixed: the reload notice no longer raises a Lua error (Reload.lua:468, arithmetic on a string) while the test mode bar shows. Before, with test mode on, a change that needs a reload, such as **Square icons**, showed no notice, and a notice already up repeated the error four times a second. Now the notice shows and stands clear of the test mode bar, and any later error in placing it is reported once while the notice stays at the top of the screen.
- Options window: the mouse wheel always scrolls the page, also over a slider. Before, a long page such as **Nameplates** or **Cast bars** stopped scrolling as soon as a slider passed under the cursor, and each wheel notch changed that setting. Now Shift and the mouse wheel step a slider, as does the wheel while its number box has the cursor, and the box shows the new value.
- Options window keys (out of a fight): Ctrl+F puts the cursor in **Search**, Escape closes an open dropdown or Yes/No question and leaves the window open (a second Escape closes it), and Enter presses the question's first button. A long dropdown, such as the fonts or textures, opens with the cursor in its filter field, which says "Type to filter". Enter there picks the first line shown and Escape closes the list.
- **Home**: each line of **Recent changes** says what the change was, from the value before to the value now, such as "Off to On", "300 ms to 450 ms" or a colour swatch with its code, in the same words as a row's reset arrow. Pointing at its **Undo** says what it puts back, such as "Puts back: Off". The reload notice's list says values in the same words.
- **Search** lights the words you typed in each result in the accent colour, and the line under its title says how many results it found ("12 results", "No results"). A **Profiles** result such as **Import profile**, **In a raid** or **Duplicate profile** now opens the Profiles page at that row and flashes it, as a setting's result does. Before, it opened the page at its top.
- The welcome screen, the ready-made looks and the "Your screen is set" card after Finish come back after a fight. Before, a fight hid them for good, with their **Reload** and **Back to** buttons. Now chat says "Flat UI: the welcome screen comes back after the fight.", and the same view, with the same result and ticks, opens again when the fight ends.
- Test mode bar: new **Move** button, which opens `/flatui move` with the samples kept on, so each frame can be placed where it shows. A double-click on the bar's title puts a dragged bar back at its own place above or under the options window. Before, a dragged bar stayed where it was dropped until a reload.
- Reload notice: **Undo** is dimmed in a fight and while Setup has the options window, where it does nothing. Before, it looked ready and then refused with a chat line. Pointing at **Reload** or **Later** now shows the list of changes too, such as "Square icons: Off now, On at login".
- **Reset page** is dimmed while every setting on the tab is at its default, and a click then says "Nothing to reset: every setting on this tab is at its default." Before, it asked "Reset every setting on this page?" and did nothing. After a reset the strip says how many settings went back, such as "Reset 5 settings on Cast bars > Size. Home's Recent changes can undo each." Pointing at the button says what it does.
- The options window fades in over about a tenth of a second and plays the game's window sounds as it opens and closes, but not when `/flatui move` hides it and brings it back. Pointing at the title bar says "Drag to move. Ctrl and the mouse wheel: Window scale, now 100%.", and each Ctrl-wheel step shows the new **Window scale** in the strip.
- **Raid frames**: with **Name colour** at **Class**, **Reaction** or **Custom**, the names on the raid frames and raid-style party frames now show exactly that colour, also while the game's own class colours for raid names are on. Before, the game's class colour was mixed into Flat UI's: a mage's name came out a dull teal instead of its class blue, a rogue's yellow turned mustard, and a Custom gold name on a shaman looked olive. **White** is now white on raid frames too. Turn the row back to **As the game has it** and the game's colour returns.
- **Low health border** is drawn in setup and test mode. In setup's **Bars, heals and borders** step, Mara drops to 15% with the border round her health bar while the row is on, in the **Low health border colour** you pick, and it goes once **Low health below** is set under 15%. In test mode, Ivo (party, a warlock at 16% after Life Tap) and two raid members at 14% show it, and Bex's border comes and goes as her drifting health crosses the limit. Before, none of these rows changed the picture, and no sample member was under 20%.
- **Low health border** now also shows on your own pet frame, the one you Mend Pet or Health Funnel, also in a fight. Before, it showed on party members' pets but never on yours.
- New **Pulse the low health border** (off), under **Status colours** on **Unit frames** > **All frames: bars and pictures**: the low health border beats softly while a member is below the limit, so it catches the eye on a full raid, and stops as they heal.
- **Pet target frame**: when the unit dies, the bar is now full and grey with **Dead** at its end, as on the raid frames. Before, the bar was empty, so the grey never showed.
- **Pet target frame** and **Druid mana in forms**: a new unit, or a bar that just appeared, shows its real value at once, and only later changes glide. Before, the pet's bar slid up from the last target's health (often 0, from the one that just died), and the druid mana bar slid from the mana it had when you last left a form, or from almost empty after test mode.
- **Pet target frame**: the unit's raid marker sits just left of the frame, as on the target, focus and boss frames, so you can see whether your pet is on the skull or has broken the sheep. Test mode's Training Dummy shows a Skull. The row is now called **Raid marker on target, focus, boss and pet target frames**.
- **Pet target frame** and the druid's mana bar take the **Corners** of the unit frames (**Square**, **Slightly soft** or **Soft**) on their border, background and fill, and change at once with the row. Before, both stayed square beside rounded bars.
- **Highlight under the mouse**: the pet target frame's bar now brightens under the mouse like every other unit frame, and stays flat with the row off. On rounded bars the highlight now takes the bar's rounded shape. Before, a square layer lit up the four corner pixels outside the rounded border, which showed most on dark backgrounds and on raid cells.
- **Nameplates**: the threat value left of an enemy's bar no longer sits under a raid marker such as Skull, and the quest mark no longer piles onto both. Before, the !, the marker and the '85%' lay on the same spot. Now the left of the bar reads ! 85% marker bar, each clear of the next, and they move out as markers come and go and the number grows, in a fight too. At **Quest mark position** **Right**, the mark now sits after the threat value and the name of the enemy's target instead of over them.
- **Nameplates**: with **Level box** at **Hidden**, a quest mark at **Right** now sits at the end of the bar instead of a box-width past it. With **Raid marker** off, a marked quest enemy's mark at **Left** no longer floats out past an invisible marker. The setup drawing now matches, and follows **Raid marker horizontal offset** and **Raid marker vertical offset** as the live plate does.
- New **Kill count beside the quest mark** (on), on the **Marks and icons** tab of **Nameplates**: a small gold count such as 3/8 sits beside the quest mark on enemies you have to kill. It counts up with each kill and goes with the mark once the objective is done. Enemies that only drop a quest item show the mark alone.
- New **Leave quest marks to Questie** (on), on the **Marks and icons** tab of **Nameplates**: while Questie is loaded, Flat UI's quest mark stays away, so quest enemies no longer show two icons. Turn it off to have both. `/flatui status` says when the marks are left to Questie.
- **Quest mark on enemies you need for a quest**: on an enemy someone else has tapped (grey health bar), the mark and its count turn grey and faint, since the kill will not count. They turn bright again if the tap drops. Marks now fade in as they appear and fade away as the objective completes, instead of popping.
- **Click through friendly nameplates** (**Size and clicks** tab of **Nameplates**) now works whether or not **Restyle friendly nameplates** is on. Before, it was greyed out and did nothing while friendly plates kept the game's look. New **Where friendly nameplates are clicked through** (**Everywhere** by default, or **In dungeons and raids**) switches as you zone in and out, after the fight if you zone during one.
- **Test mode**: the sample nameplate now takes You want, Changing hands and You do not want in turn every 3 seconds. Each state shows its threat ring as **Threat glow** asks, a sample threat value (40%, 92%, 110%) at **Threat value position**, size, offsets, outline and colour, and the fade of **Fade enemies held for you**, so every row can be seen as it changes. Before, only the health bar colour changed. It also shows the quest mark's kill count.
- **Overpower on enemy nameplates**: for a warrior, the setup's nameplate marks drawing and test mode's sample nameplate now show the Overpower icon at **Overpower icon size**, with its swipe draining over 5 seconds and starting again while **Time left on the Overpower icon** is on. The setup step 'Target, class icons and raid marker' is now called **Marks and icons**, like the tab.
- **Channel ticks on the player bar** show in every fight. Before, wherever the game protects your cast bar in a fight (Edit Mode's Lock to player frame is one such layout), a Mind Flay, Drain Life or Blizzard cast in a fight showed no tick marks at all, and if your first channel of the session started in a fight none were made until you channelled out of one. Now the marks are made out of a fight and only change width while you channel, so they show and follow pushback in every fight and every Edit Mode layout. `/flatui status` counts any channel that still showed none.
- **Channel ticks**: each mark fades to a faint line as its tick lands, and the next one stays bright. In the middle of Blizzard or Hellfire, the ticks left and the moment to stop are plain to see, where before every mark looked the same until the channel ended. Setup draws the passed marks dim too.
- **Combat text** preview, in setup and in the options window's preview band: it now draws the **Outgoing** scroll area beside the numbers on you, a white swing with its icon, a yellow spell crit with its icon and a Dodge. The column climbs or falls, arcs or slides as **Outgoing style** and **Outgoing direction** say, shows or drops its icons with **Spell icons**, loses its oldest number at **Fast** **Speed**, and hides while **Where your hits show** is **Over enemies**. Before, these rows changed nothing in the picture.
- New **Size of the Outgoing area** (Same as numbers over enemies), on the **Scroll areas** tab of **Combat text**. **Same as your text** or **Smaller than numbers over enemies** (three quarters) keep big numbers over the enemy with a calmer column beside you. Before, your hits there were always the size of the numbers over enemies, which is still the default.
- **No target warning** no longer warns a healer for a friendly target. While you heal (your group role, else most talent points in a healing tree, else a priest), a living friendly target counts as a target, so a holy priest or restoration druid who targets the tank in a fight no longer gets the red No target every time. Damage dealers keep the warning while **Warn with a friendly target** is on. `/flatui status` says when friendly targets count.
- **No target** now breathes gently while it shows, from full to about half opacity and back every 1.2 seconds, so it is harder to stop seeing in a busy fight. **+Combat** and **-Combat** start a little larger and settle in 0.15 seconds, like the pop of the scroll-area numbers. Before, both appeared abruptly and stood still.
- Picking a sound for **No target sound**, **Entering combat sound** or **Leaving combat sound** in the options window plays it once, so you can choose by ear. **None** plays nothing, and nothing plays in a fight.
- New **Queued next melee ability** (on) with **Queued ability colour** (orange), under **Next melee ability** on the **Swing timers** page. While Heroic Strike, Cleave, Maul or Raptor Strike waits for your next swing, the main hand timer turns that colour, and it takes its own colour back as the swing lands, so a late or forgotten queue shows at a glance. Setup draws it on the main hand timer.
- New **Finish flash** (In the cast colour), on the **Colours** page. A finished cast or channel on a flat cast bar or a nameplate cast bar now glows in the bar's own colour, where before it ended with the game's gold or green flash over a class-coloured or interrupt-coloured bar. **The game's** brings that flash back, and **Off** removes it.
- Test mode's sample player cast bar now matches the real bar in every phase. With **Latency zone** on, the red zone moves to the left end during the Drain Life channel, and with **Cast time on your bars** on, the seconds go during the Interrupted phase. Before, the zone stayed at the right end and the seconds stayed on screen.
- **Proc art** box in `/flatui move`: moving it or putting it back no longer raises a Lua error once the art has been drawn (with Samples on, or after any proc). The art now follows the box as soon as you let go.
- **Fading**: with Flat UI's action buttons, **Main action bar** and **Action bar 2** to **8** now fade Flat UI's own bars, also while **Leave the game's action bars alone** is on, as it is by default. Before, those rows faded nothing until that row was off. The game's hidden bars are never touched, also while Quick Keybind Mode shows them.
- **Cast fill animation** and **Interrupt flash** on Flat UI's buttons now also light a macro that casts the spell, such as /cast Frostbolt, and an item with a cast, such as the Hearthstone or a bandage, as the game's buttons do. Before, only a button that held the spell itself filled.
- **Cast fill animation** follows pushback: when you are hit while casting, the fill tracks the real cast time, where before it reached the top early and sat there. A channel cut short empties at its real pace. Channels now fill in the cast bar's **Channel colour** (green by default) instead of the **Cast colour**.
- **Hotkey position**: **Top left**, **Top**, **Centre**, **Bottom left**, **Bottom** and **Bottom right** now put the key in that corner or in the middle. Before, a short key such as "1" stayed near the right, because the game's hotkey box is right-justified.
- **Hotkey size** and **Macro name size** above about 120% no longer cut the text off: the text gets room for its size.
- Tooltips of Flat UI's action buttons update while you point at them, five times a second, as the game's buttons do. "Cooldown remaining" counts down, and lines such as not enough mana or out of range change as they change. Before, the tooltip stayed as it was when you first pointed at the button.
- **Flash when ready** no longer flashes early when you press another ability in the last moments of a cooldown. The flash now waits until the button really is ready, when its sweep ends.
- New **Cooldown number colour** and **Tenths below** (0 s, never, by default) on the **Colours and cooldowns** page under **Action bars**. They set the colour of the cooldown numbers on Flat UI's buttons and the game's, and show tenths under that many seconds (for example 2.4). **Cooldown number size** set back to **As the game has it** now applies at once, with no /reload.
- **Bar block**: new **Full opacity with a target** (off). It brings a faded block back to full while you have a target, for example for a pull with a ranged opener or a buff on a friend, with the same quick return and eased fade.
- **Quick Keybind Mode**: when a fight starts with it open, one chat line now says that your action buttons are back for the fight and that the game's show again when it ends. If the switch cannot happen in time, the line says the game's buttons stay for this fight. Before, the buttons changed under the open keybind window without a word.
- New **Tracker tooltips** (**Out of combat only** by default; also **On** and **Off**), under **Clicks** on the **Trackers** page. Pointing at a tracker on screen shows its name and kind, then a coloured line with its state: Up in green, Low in amber, Missing in red. The line gives the time left and charges, or how many are in your bags or arrows left. A group buff adds who lacks it, such as "2 of 5 lack it (Mara, Eli)", and a dim "Out of combat, a click fixes it" shows where **Click to apply** covers the tracker. In a fight it says when the game hides the buff and its own icon shows it, or gives the last known state. A click still goes through to the world. Before, a tracker had a tooltip only out of combat while a click could fix it, and it only said what the click does; that tooltip now starts with the same lines.
- **Trackers**: **Row direction** also offers **Column, downward** and **Column, upward**. They stack the trackers' icons, bars or text under each other, left-aligned, the usual layout for timer bars. The column sits above or below the personal resource display, beside the character or at its `/flatui move` spot, as the row does. Before, five bars lay side by side in a strip about 790 pixels wide across the screen. Flat UI setup still draws a column as a row.
- New **Sound when it turns low** (off), on the **Low and missing** tab of **Trackers** and per tracker in **Edit a tracker** (`/flatui tracker set 1 lowsound on`). It plays the kit of **Sound** once as a tracker goes from fine to low: a buff at its **Low at**, arrows under **Ammunition minimum**, or a pet set to Passive. The game hides buffs in a fight, so a buff turns low by sound between fights. Before, only a tracker going missing played a sound, though setup said low too; setup's text for **Sound when it drops** now says missing.
- **Trackers**: a tracker set to **Only when low or missing** now fades in over 0.2 seconds when it turns low or missing, and out over 0.25 seconds once you fix it. Before, it popped on and off. Test mode and a pet tracker shown by its pet's health still change at once.
- New **Buff tooltips** (**On** by default; also **Out of combat only** and **Off**) on the **Defensives on you** page. Pointing at a Pain Suppression, Blessing of Protection or Power Word: Shield icon now shows the game's own buff tooltip, as on **Your buffs**, and a click still goes through to the world. Before, the row took no mouse at all.
- **Defensives on you**: the samples in test mode and `/flatui test icons` now look like the real icons. They follow **Icon border**, Borderless and the border colour; before, they always had a teal border. With **Caster's name** on, Eli, Mara and Ivo show under them in priest, paladin and warlock colours, so the preview has the room the names take. The icons shown out of combat when the game makes no icon row also lose the teal border.
- **Absorb shields**: when a shield breaks, the red flash now covers the whole stack as it was, the total and every shield, and fades out over 0.4 seconds. Before, a red block covered only the top bar and vanished at once, so a shield lower down never flashed when it broke.
- **Your buffs**: the samples in test mode and `/flatui test icons` now show **Time left** the way the row draws it. **Under the icon** puts the seconds under each sample in the row's small font, and **Thin bar under the icon** a thin blue bar in the **Bar texture** that runs down with the sweep. **On the icon** keeps the countdown, and **Off** shows the sweep alone. Before, the samples always showed the countdown on the icon.
- **Cooldown pulse**: with **Grow as it fades** on and Borderless off, the border now swells with the icon, because its edges are drawn on the pulse itself.
- New **Opacity** (70% by default, 30 to 100%) on the **Cooldown pulse** tab of **Cooldowns** sets how solid a pulse is at its brightest. Before, a pulse never went above 70%. An item's name under the icon now shows in its quality's colour (green, blue, purple); spells and test pulses stay white.
- **Threat list**: your own bar no longer drops off the list when you rank below the last bar. With **Number of bars** below the size of a raid or group, the last bar becomes yours, with its white outline and its red pulse, and your place before your name, for example "12. Jon". Before, a damage dealer in 6th place or lower never saw their bar, and **Warn near aggro** could play its sound with no bar pulsing. On a boss, where the game hides threat, your bar keeps the group's order and has no number. New **Always show your bar** (on/off).
- **Threat list**: tanks no longer hear the near-aggro warning. Before, a tank who lost an enemy sat at 90-99% without tanking, so **Warn near aggro** pulsed red and played the raid warning, and an off-tank in a raid heard it on every add the main tank held. While your role is tank (your group role, else your stance or form) that warning is off. Instead, when an enemy you held turns to someone else, your bar pulses red for 2 seconds and **Warning sound** plays once. **Warning sound** now serves both warnings. Not on bosses, where the game hides threat. New **Warn when you lose aggro** (on/off).
- **Threat list** and **Healer mana** fade in and out. Before, they snapped on at the first event of a pull and off the moment the fight ended or the group changed. Showing again during a fade-out stops it at once, so nothing flickers between pulls.
- **Healer mana**: the bar of a healer out of your range fades, also in a fight, and comes back as they close in. Before, a healer 60 yards behind the group showed a full bright bar. In test mode the sample Shaman shows faded. New **Dim healers out of range** (on/off).
- **Party cooldowns** and **Enemy cooldowns**: an icon's tooltip counts down live. Before, it wrote "Ready in 1:42" once and froze; it still said "Ready in 0:00" after the cooldown ended, and kept the old name when another ability moved into the slot. Now the time counts down every second, turns to the green **Ready** as the sweep ends, and follows the icon's ability.
- **Ping bar**: each button shows the key you bound under **Flat UI** in the game's Key Bindings, short as on action bars ("M4", "s-F", "N1"), in its top right corner, and it changes as soon as you rebind. Before, you had to point at a button and read the tooltip. New **Keys on the buttons** (on/off).
- **Ping bar**: the line that names the waiting ping ("Attack: click where to ping...") now stays on screen. Before, it was centred under the bar, so with the bar at a screen edge, or laid out vertically, half of it was off screen. Now it hangs inward from the bar, or beside the bar when it is vertical. The bar is also explicitly protected, so the click that places, switches or cancels a waiting ping keeps working in a fight.
- **Group tools**: each raid marker's border is in its marker's colour (star yellow, circle orange, diamond purple, triangle green, moon silver-blue, square blue, cross red, skull white), so the skull and the cross no longer look alike at a glance. The tooltips of the markers and of the clear name the game's own marker key ("Key: ...") when you bound one. New **Borders in the marker's colour** (on/off).
- **Group tools** in test mode: the ready check tick shows a sample tally of 4/5 in amber, and its tooltip names a sample Eli as not ready. The pull button counts down from **Countdown length** in amber, again and again, without sending a countdown to your group. Before, test mode never showed either. A real ready check or countdown takes their place.
- **Bags**: the bag menu (**Assign To**, and **Ignore** for **Clean Up** and **Junk Selling**) can now be reached in **Sections**, the default view. Right-click a bag in the **Bag slots** row, in either view, for that bag's settings, and in **Bag by bag** a right-click on a bag's label opens them as a click does. Before, only a left click on a label in Bag by bag opened the menu, and Sections had no way to it. The slot's tooltip says what the bag is assigned to, and whether clean up or Sell junk leaves it alone.
- **Bags**: point at a folded section's title to see what it holds. Each item gets one line with its icon, its name in its quality's colour and its total count at the right (Linen Cloth x40), with stacks of the same item added up and the largest first. The list stops at 12 lines, then says how many more there are. This works in both windows and for your other characters. Before, the tooltip only said to click to open it.
- **Bags**: a click on a bag in the **Bag slots** row now shows only that bag. Every other slot in the window dims (the search still applies) and the slot's edge takes the **Highlight colour** until you click it again, click another bag or close the window. This also works in a fight. To pick a bag up, drag it, as on the game's bag bar. Before (since 0.5.33) a plain click lifted the bag onto the cursor. A click with an item or a bag on the cursor still puts it in.
- **Bags**: at the banker, pointing at a bank bag slot in the bank window now dims every bank slot that is not in that bag, as the **Bag slots** row does in the bag window. Its tooltip adds how many of the bag's slots are free (12 of 16 slots free).
- **Bags**: new search term tip:, which looks inside each item's tooltip. tip:stamina, tip:"fire resistance" or tip:"chance to hit" lights every item whose tooltip says it, and it combines with the other terms, as in tip:agility #boe q>=uncommon. The search box's help lists it.
- **Bags**: the **Bag slots** row now behaves like the game's bag bar. While the bag on your cursor fits a slot, that slot stays lit, and a slot whose bag you picked up turns grey until you put the bag down.
- **Bags**: with the bag window open, loot you get shows its icon on the slot of its bag in the **Bag slots** row, larger at first, then shrinking into the slot as it fades, in about half a second. You can see it go into the backpack, the soul bag or the quiver.
- **Bags**: Shift-click a section's title to open only that section and fold all the others; Shift-click it again to open them all. A section's right-click menu has a new **Fold every section** line while any section is open, beside **Unfold every section**. The title's tooltip says what a Shift-click does.
- **Bags**: with **Deposit and Take out** off, a section's right-click menu at the banker no longer offers **Put <section> in the bank** or **Take <section> out**, and its tooltip no longer says to right-click for that.
- **Bags**: pointing at an empty bag slot, on the game's bag bar or in the **Bag slots** row, no longer dims the whole bag window, free slots included, as if your bags were empty. Only a slot that holds a bag lights its items.
- In a fight, a click on the **Time** line by the minimap (the stopwatch, or the calendar with Shift), on the reputation bar (your reputations) or on the quest count (the quest log) no longer does nothing: chat says once "Flat UI: the stopwatch opens when the fight ends." (or the calendar, your reputations, the quest log), the window opens as the fight ends, and in a fight the tooltip hints read "..., after the fight".
- The lines of **Text around the map** (coordinates, durability, frame rate, time) now show their tooltip in a fight too, and it stays up to date as you walk, where before it showed nothing in a fight and froze on old coordinates. **Tooltips in combat** at **Hide every tooltip** on the **Tooltips** page still hides it.
- New **Text size** (100%, 70% to 160%) under **Text around the map** on the **Minimap** page sizes the four lines by the map at once; the addon button row under the map and the room under the zone name follow.
- Shift-clicking the coordinates or the experience bar with the macro editor open no longer writes "/cast ..." and a line break into the macro: the text goes only into an open chat box, or into one Flat UI opens.
- New **Animate gains** (on) on the **Experience and menu bars** page: Flat UI's experience and reputation bar fill now slides up to its new value over about 0.4 s at each kill or hand-in, and the stretch you just gained lights up softly and fades; a new level, another faction or a loss still sets the bar at once.
- Flat UI's experience bar now writes large numbers as the game does: 12,150 / 27,000 in the bar text (**Value and percent**, **Amount to go**), in the tooltip (Experience, Rested, reputation) and in the Shift-click chat line.
- New **Experience bar** (off) under **What fades** on the **Fading** page fades Flat UI's experience and reputation bar with **Faded opacity**, **Wait before fading** and **Fade time**, back to full under the mouse and as **Stay visible** says; while block 1 of the **Bar block** page holds it with **Fade the block** on, it fades with the block.
- The **Objective tracker** rule of **Fading** now also fades Questie's tracker while Questie is loaded; under the mouse it comes back, and the tracker's **Opacity in combat** still wins in a fight with no flash to full afterwards.
- **Quest count** on the tracker's Quests heading now answers the mouse: pointing at the 12/20 shows the **Quest log** data panel's tooltip (quests, "Your quest log is full.", ready to hand in and their experience) with a dim "Click: open the quest log", and a click opens the quest log.
- New **When** (Always, Only in a fight) under **Hide error messages** on the **Messages and timers** page: at Only in a fight every message shows out of combat. New kind **Not in line of sight** (off) hides "Target not in line of sight".
- **Spell tooltips**: new **Next rank** (on/off). A spell with more ranks ends with dim "Next rank: Rank 6 at level 32", or green "Rank 6: you can learn it now" once your level reaches it. Nothing shows at the top rank, once you know a higher rank, or on talents, pet spells and judgements. Before, only a visit to the trainer told you a new rank was waiting.
- **Tooltips**: new **Item level** (on/off). A weapon or a piece of armour says "Item level 63" in gold under the game's own lines, on quest rewards, chat links and loot roll items too. The comparison tooltips beside an item say theirs, so the levels stand side by side. Before, item levels showed only on bag and character slots.
- **Chat text**: new **Channel names** (**Number and name** by default, or **As the game has it**). General, Trade, LocalDefense and the other numbered channels read "[2. Trade]" and "[1. General]" where they read "[2. Trade - City]" and "[1. General - Elwynn Forest]". Recent chat shown again after a reload reads the same way. A click on the label still opens the channel, and a window that would no longer take a shortened line keeps the long name.
- **Chat**: new **Arrow keys without Alt** (on/off). While you type, Left and Right move the cursor and Up and Down bring back the last 32 lines you sent. Before, the arrow keys turned your character unless you held Alt. Off, the game's behaviour comes back.
- **Show durability** on the Character window: the line turns red while any item is broken, even when the average is high, and at least yellow while any item is at 20% or less, as the minimap's durability line warns. Before, one broken weapon among whole gear read a white "Durability 92%". Its tooltip opens with a red "1 item broken: no stats until repaired" and lists each item with its icon and its name in quality colour, without brackets. Where the game keeps gear from wearing out, the line is gone, as the minimap's is.
- **Health text on the bar** (tooltips): the text grows and shrinks with **Health bar height** and is left off bars under 5 px, where it used to spill over the tooltip's edge. A dead unit reads Dead instead of 0%, and the last text stays while the tooltip fades instead of going blank. **Health bar height** and **Health text on the bar** grey out while **Hide the health bar** is on.
- **Tooltips**: new **Who in your group targets it** (on/off). In a party or raid a grey "Targeted by: Mara, Eli, Ivo" line under a unit's tooltip names the members on it, in class colours, at most six then +N, so you can see who is still on the wrong mob. On **Target of units**, "You" turns red while a unit you can attack targets you. Solo, nothing changes.
- **Tooltips of chat links** now covers quest links too: pointing at a [Quest name] a party member linked shows its level, text and objectives, as items, spells and achievements already did. It never shows in a fight.
- **Show thorns**: the line shows only while something deals damage to attackers (a Thorns, Retribution Aura or Fire Shield buff, an item, or a shield spike), and comes back as soon as such a buff lands. Before, every character without one had a grey "Thorns 0" on the Character window all the time. New **Also at 0** (on/off, off by default) keeps the grey line.
- **Chat and tooltips** and **Windows**: their hooks on the game's functions now go through Flat UI's shared hook path, so a fault in one no longer stops the others on the same function. Nothing changes on screen.
- **Mark the reward worth most** (**Errands**, **Quests and levelling**): the gold coin now shows as soon as a quest's offer or reward page with a choice of rewards opens. Before, it showed only after the game updated an item of the window or after you toggled the row, so most windows had no coin.
- **Mark the reward worth most**: the coin is now a small gold coin with a soft glow on the top right corner of the item's icon, clear of the item's name and of the game's own reward icon. It takes the quest window's size, so it fits when Flat UI's window size makes the window 80% or 150%, and it no longer draws over a bag window lying on the quest window. Before, it sat at a fixed 14 px over the end of the item's name, where a long name ran under it.
- **Data panels**: new **Experience** choice for a slot. It shows "XP 45.2%  +12%", the rested share in blue. Its tooltip has your experience over what the level needs, what is left to level, your rested experience, the experience of the quests ready to hand in, and Flat UI's time to level and pace. Shift-click puts "Level 34: 12,345 / 27,000 (45%), 1h 20m to level" in chat. At the top level, or with experience turned off, the slot takes no room.
- **Data panels**: more slots take a click, and the dim last line of each tooltip says what it does. **Time** opens the stopwatch and alarm (Shift-click: the calendar), **Quest log** the quest log, **Friends online** the friends list, **Guild online** the guild window and **Durability** the character window, all out of a fight. Shift-click on **Coordinates** puts "Elwynn Forest 45.2, 61.8" in chat, in a fight too. Before, only **Bag space** and **Equipment set** took a left click. With **Tooltips** off, these slots keep their clicks.
- **Data panels**: the **Durability** tooltip ends with **Repair cost**, what repairing your worn gear will cost, out of a fight. You know the bill before you reach a merchant.
- **Data panels**: the **Gold** tooltip's **This session** line also says what the session made or spent an hour, as in "+3g 12s (1g 4s an hour)", once the session has run 10 minutes. It counts the same session as the bag window's gold line, and a /reload keeps it.
- **Data panels**: the slot under the mouse gets a faint light behind it, as Flat UI's own buttons light up, and keeps it while its menu (**Equipment set**) is open. Before, pointing at a slot changed nothing on the panel.
- New **Turn on what the glow needs** (**World and cursor** page, under **Glow on the nearest object**). One pick, out of a fight, ticks the game's **Enable Interact Key** and sets its **Interact Key Icons** to **Show All**, the two game options the glow needs, and a chat line says what it changed. Once for your account, a login with the glow on and the Interact Key off brings one chat line pointing to it. Before, the glow drew nothing until you found both game options yourself, and only `/flatui status` said why. The glow's tip no longer calls it golden, since **Glow colour** can colour it by what the object is.
- **Put new ranks on your bars**: the "New ranks for 4 buttons" line with **Uprank and reload** has a **New spell ranks line** box in `/flatui move`, so you can place it. Test mode shows a sample of it, "New ranks for 3 buttons: Frostbolt, Fireball and 1 more.", with **Uprank and reload** greyed. Before, it always sat at the top of the screen, 186 px down, and you saw it only after training.
- **Lava tick timer**: dodges in a row are counted. The bar says **Dodged x2**, **Dodged x3** as a streak builds, and a hit starts it again. `/flatui lava` names your best streak of the session. The test mode sample shows the count too, and on a narrow bar the word no longer runs under the timer's count. Before, every dodge said the same **Dodged**.

## 0.5.34

- For WoW: Forever 1.60.1 build 70938. The interface number stays 16001.
- Test mode samples no longer show through the options window: before, the sample **Entering Combat** and **Leaving Combat** alerts, the sample cast bar and the cooldown pulse icon drew inside the window over its rows when it was dragged over them; now the window, and the welcome screen above it, stay in front of every sample and HUD frame, and the rows stay readable.
- The sample unit frames of test mode and of the setup's drawings show real pictures in place of a grey placeholder that looked like a floppy disk: a class icon on party members and on the humanoid target (a mage's on Defias Pillager), a wolf's icon on the pet **Wolf** and on the focus **Gruff Swiftbite**, beast icons on the party pets, the Voidwalker's icon on the warlock's pet, and your own face on the target of target, which is you.
- **Movers**: the game's windows open clear of your unit frames. Before, the Character window (also on its Reputation and Skills tabs), the Spellbook, the map and quest log and Professions opened at the game's spot near the top left, and their title bar covered the lower part of the player and target frames, mana bars included. Now a window the game would open over them opens just under the lower of the two, as far as the screen leaves room, and any window beside it moves down with it so they stay in a row. New **Open windows under the unit frames** (on), in the **Windows** section of **Movers**, turns this off. A window you drag stays where you put it, a unit frame you moved lower down leaves the windows where the game puts them, and a window the game protects stays at the game's spot if it opens in a fight.
- **Windows**: the **Spellbook** has dark flat pages with light text, like the other restyled windows. Before, it kept the game's parchment pages inside Flat UI's dark window. Now the parchment, the bookmark, the turning page corner and the header plates are gone, spell names are white, the lines under them grey, and the headers and page number gold, on every page you turn to. New **Spellbook parchment** (off), under **Window art** on the **Windows** page, brings back the parchment and its dark text exactly as the game draws them.
- **Minimap** page: **Coordinates** no longer shows your place twice. While the game's own coordinates show under the map (the game's Options, Interface, Coordinates), Flat UI's line stays hidden, the other lines start under the game's, and `/flatui status` says why. Before, the same "42.7, 23.3" stood on two lines under the map. Turn the game's coordinates off to get Flat UI's line back, with its tooltip and Shift-click.
- **Experience and menu bars**: Flat UI's experience and reputation bars now draw their thin border while **Bar border** is on (on by default), also with **Borderless** on, as it is by default. The border goes around the background, which stays in the **Background colour** and **Background opacity** of the Flat UI page. Before, Borderless took the border away, so at low experience only the short fill showed and the empty part was almost invisible. Now the whole bar shows from end to end, and setup draws it the same way.
- `/flatui move`: the name on each box is one line cut short with "..." to the box's width, and a box too small for a line shows no name. The small boxes in the middle of the screen (**Cast bar**, **Cooldown pulse**, **Loss of control**, **Combat text** and others) no longer pile their names on top of each other. Point at a box to see it drawn over every other box, with its whole name on a plate; the smaller box under the cursor still takes the mouse. Before, long names ran out of their boxes and could not be read.
- **Hit table under the player frame** (**Hit table** page) no longer runs into the pet frame. At the spot every new profile starts with (and at any spot of your own), a hunter's or warlock's pet frame, and test mode's sample pet frame "Wolf", covered the right side of the table. Now the table steps down under the pet frame and its auras while one shows, and goes back up when the pet goes. `/flatui status` says when it has stepped down.
- **Bags**: the **Bag slots** row over the bag window's footer sat right under the keyring with nothing between them, so the bag icons looked like more keyring items. It now stands a little apart under a thin line, and each bag shows its free slots in its corner, red when the bag is full. The bank window's row of bank bag slots stands apart under the same line.
- **Bags**: in **Bag by bag**, bags with the same name had labels that could not be told apart, such as three Journeyman's Backpacks. Each one now adds its number, for example "Journeyman's Backpack · bag 3". In the bank window these are bank bags 1 to 8.

## 0.5.33

- For WoW: Forever 1.60.1 build 70938. The interface number stays 16001.
- **Ping bar**: click a ping, then click where. A click on a button no longer pings at once: the cursor turns into the game's ping cursor, a line by the bar says which ping waits, and the next left click anywhere on the screen pings that spot for your group, also in a fight; right-click or Esc cancels. Before, a click with no target pinged the world behind the bar itself, where the cursor was (often the sky, which the game refuses), so the bar seemed to do nothing. **Shift-click** pings your target at once, as a click did before (with no target it works as a click). The keys under **Flat UI** in the game's Key Bindings now ping what is under the mouse at once, as the game's own ping keys do, even with a target: hover the spot, press the key. **With no target** is gone, as neither a click nor a key uses it now. The tooltips say what a click, a Shift-click and the key do.
- **Defaults** follow the author's current **Default** profile, so what you never set changes. On the **Combat text** page, **Incoming style** is **Straight** (it was **Arc**), **Speed** is **Slow** (it was **Normal**), the classic style's **Rise distance** is 175 (it was 100), **Where friendly numbers show** is **Both**, party and raid frames and friendly nameplates (it was **Off**), **Healing over friendly units** is **Healing you do** (it was **Healing they take from anyone**), and the **No target warning** is on. A new install's **Default** profile and every new profile start with the author's current positions, fitted to your screen as before: to the eight of 0.5.29 (the tracked buff icons, the absorb shields, the action bar block, the cooldown icons, the loot rolls, the party and raid frames, the experience bar and the main hand swing timer) come the raid frames, the bag and bank windows, both combat text areas, the group tools and the hit table; the other frames start at the game's or Flat UI's own place. Picking **Jon Default** among the ready-made looks sets the same. A profile that saved its own value for one of these rows keeps it, and a profile you have keeps its positions.
- **Reload notice**: it no longer sits on top of the test mode bar or the `/flatui move` toolbar.
